However, supervision is typically provided only by the final outcome, which makes it difficult to evaluate the quality of memory updates at each turn in the multi-turn training setting. This introduces a temporal credit assignment challenge. Existing approaches, such as LLM-as-a-judge or process reward models, incur substantial computational overhead and suffer from estimation noise. To better address the credit assignment problem in multi-turn memory training, we propose Teacher-Aligned Reward Reshaping for Multi-Turn Reinforcement Learning (TAMTRL). TAMTRL leverages relevant documents as teacher signals by aligning them with each turn of model input and assigns rewards through normalized probabilities in a self-supervised manner. This provides fine-grained learning signals for each memory update and improves long-context processing. Experiments with multiple models of varying scales across seven long-context benchmarks show that TAMTRL consistently outperforms strong baselines, demonstrating its effectiveness. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TAMTRL-F1F8.

</details>


### [257] [Probing How Scalable Table Data Enhances General Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21719)
*Huaibing Xie,Guoliang Zhao,Yang Liu,Shihan Dou,Siming Huang,Yanling Xiao,Shaolei Wang,Yiting Liu,Cheng Zhang,Shaofan Liu,Pluto Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用结构化表格数据提升大语言模型的长上下文推理能力，通过理论分析和实验证明表格数据具有显著优势，并设计了可扩展的数据合成方法TableLong，在多个基准上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索哪些数据类型对长上下文推理有效及其原因，特别是发现结构化表格数据具有周期性依赖结构，可能有助于缓解长上下文中依赖消失的问题。

Method: 基于互信息对表格数据的依赖结构进行数学分析，提出TableLong方法用于生成高质量、多样且可验证的结构化表格数据，并通过强化学习进行训练。

Result: 实验表明，使用表格数据训练后，大语言模型在多个长上下文基准上平均提升8.24%，在跨领域基准上平均提升8.06%。

Conclusion: 结构化表格数据是增强大语言模型长上下文推理能力的有效数据类型，其周期性依赖结构是关键因素，为后续高效后训练数据设计提供了实践指导。

Abstract: As real-world tasks grow increasingly complex, long-context reasoning has become a core capability for Large Language Models (LLMs). However, few studies explore which data types are effective for long-context reasoning and why. We find that structured table data with periodic structures shows strong potential for long-context reasoning. Motivated by this observation, we mathematically analyze tabular dependency structures using mutual information, revealing periodic non-vanishing dependencies in table data. Furthermore, we systematically analyze the capabilities of structured table data, conduct relevant scaling experiments, and validate its underlying mechanisms for enhancing long-context reasoning, yielding several meaningful insights. Leveraging these insights, we propose a simple yet scalable pipeline(TableLong) for synthesizing high-quality, diverse, and verifiable structured table data to boost long-context reasoning via RL. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that table data significantly enhances the long-context reasoning capability of LLMs across multiple long-context benchmarks (+8.24\% on average), and even improves performance on out-of-domain benchmarks (+8.06\% on average). We hope that our insights provide practical guidance for effective post-training data to enhance long-context reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


### [258] [SemEval-2026 Task 12: Abductive Event Reasoning: Towards Real-World Event Causal Inference for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21720)
*Pengfei Cao,Mingxuan Yang,Yubo Chen,Chenlong Zhang,Mingxuan Liu,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SemEval-2026任务12：溯因事件推理（AER），旨在通过支持性证据识别目标事件最合理的直接原因，填补了现实世界因果推理研究的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中事件发生的原因理解对自然语言处理和实际决策至关重要，但在证据丰富的环境中，直接因果推理仍研究不足。

Method: 将AER构建成一个基于证据的多项选择基准，包含分布式证据、间接背景因素和语义相关但非因果干扰项等挑战，并组织共享任务收集参与者结果。

Result: 该任务吸引了122名参与者，共提交518次结果，成功构建了一个聚焦于现实事件溯因推理的基准。

Conclusion: AER为现实事件的溯因推理提供了一个集中的基准，突出了未来在因果推理和多文档理解方面的挑战。

Abstract: Understanding why real-world events occur is important for both natural language processing and practical decision-making, yet direct-cause inference remains underexplored in evidence-rich settings. To address this gap, we organized SemEval-2026 Task 12: Abductive Event Reasoning (AER).\footnote{The task data is available at https://github.com/sooo66/semeval2026-task12-dataset.git} The task asks systems to identify the most plausible direct cause of a target event from supporting evidence. We formulate AER as an evidence-grounded multiple-choice benchmark that captures key challenges of real-world causal reasoning, including distributed evidence, indirect background factors, and semantically related but non-causal distractors. The shared task attracted 122 participants and received 518 submissions. This paper presents the task formulation, dataset construction pipeline, evaluation setup, and system results. AER provides a focused benchmark for abductive reasoning over real-world events and highlights challenges for future work on causal reasoning and multi-document understanding.

</details>


### [259] [Politics of Questions in News: A Mixed-Methods Study of Interrogative Stances as Markers of Voice and Power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21823)
*Bros Victor,Barbini Matilde,Gerard Patrick,Gatica-Perez Daniel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过结合计算方法与语义语用理论，对2023年至2024年法语数字新闻中超过一百万篇文章中的疑问句使用进行大规模分析，揭示疑问句在新闻话语中的功能模式、文本回应机制及主体个性化特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多局限于小规模或英语语料，缺乏对疑问句功能的细粒度区分，本文旨在填补大规模非英语新闻中疑问句使用及其社会语用意义的研究空白。

Method: 采用混合方法：首先通过自动化手段识别疑问立场、推断其功能类型并定位文内回答；随后结合基于语义与语用理论的手工标注子语料库，对量化结果进行质性验证与解释。

Result: 发现疑问句虽稀少但具有系统性分布，主要用于引入或组织议题，多数保留在信息寻求或回声式用法，引导性或附加问句罕见；大多数疑问在同一篇文章内得到回应，常以记者叙述 voice 出现；疑问语境中多出现具体个人、机构和地点，体现显著的个性化倾向。

Conclusion: 疑问立场、文本承接与声音来源可在语料库规模上操作化，结合计算语言学与语用学、社会学视角有助于深入理解当代新闻话语中提问实践的结构作用。

Abstract: Interrogatives in news discourse have been examined in linguistics and conversation analysis, but mostly in broadcast interviews and relatively small, often English-language corpora, while large-scale computational studies of news rarely distinguish interrogatives from declaratives or differentiate their functions. This paper brings these strands together through a mixed-methods study of the "Politics of Questions" in contemporary French-language digital news. Using over one million articles published between January 2023 and June 2024, we automatically detect interrogative stances, approximate their functional types, and locate textual answers when present, linking these quantitative measures to a qualitatively annotated subcorpus grounded in semantic and pragmatic theories of questions. Interrogatives are sparse but systematically patterned: they mainly introduce or organize issues, with most remaining cases being information-seeking or echo-like, while explicitly leading or tag questions are rare. Although their density and mix vary across outlets and topics, our heuristic suggests that questions are overwhelmingly taken up within the same article and usually linked to a subsequent answer-like span, most often in the journalist's narrative voice and less often through quoted speech. Interrogative contexts are densely populated with named individuals, organizations, and places, whereas publics and broad social groups are mentioned much less frequently, suggesting that interrogative discourse tends to foreground already prominent actors and places and thus exhibits strong personalization. We show how interrogative stance, textual uptake, and voice can be operationalized at corpus scale, and argue that combining computational methods with pragmatic and sociological perspectives can help account for how questioning practices structure contemporary news discourse.

</details>


### [260] [Instruction Set and Language for Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21836)
*Ezequiel Lopez-Rubio,Mario Pascual-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出IsalSR框架，通过紧凑的两层字母表将表达式DAG编码为字符串，并计算剪枝后的规范字符串，消除符号回归中的结构冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决符号回归中表达式DAG存在多种节点编号方式导致的结构冗余问题，减少搜索空间和无效的适应度评估。

Method: 设计一种名为IsalSR的表示框架，将表达式DAG转换为字符串形式，并生成一个剪枝后的规范字符串，作为完整的有标签DAG同构不变量，合并所有等价表示。

Result: 实现了对等价表达式的统一表示，有效压缩了搜索空间，减少了重复的适应度评估。

Conclusion: IsalSR能够有效消除符号回归中的结构冗余，提升搜索效率和多样性。

Abstract: A fundamental but largely unaddressed obstacle in Symbolic regression (SR) is structural redundancy: every expression DAG with admits many distinct node-numbering schemes that all encode the same expression, each occupying a separate point in the search space and consuming fitness evaluations without adding diversity. We present IsalSR (Instruction Set and Language for Symbolic Regression), a representation framework that encodes expression DAGs as strings over a compact two-tier alphabet and computes a pruned canonical string -- a complete labeled-DAG isomorphism invariant -- that collapses all the equivalent representations into a single canonical form.

</details>


### [261] [Select, Label, Evaluate: Active Testing in NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21840)
*Antonio Purificato,Maria Sofia Bucarelli,Andrea Bacciu,Amin Mantrach,Fabrizio Silvestri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了在自然语言处理中形式化主动测试（Active Testing）的方法，通过选择最具信息量的测试样本进行标注，在保证模型性能估计准确性的前提下显著减少标注成本，实验显示最多可减少95%的标注量且误差控制在1%以内，并提出了一种自适应停止准则以自动确定最优样本数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于对低错误率和高质量标签的严格要求，自然语言处理中的测试数据标注尤其昂贵，传统方法需要标注整个测试集，导致资源消耗巨大，因此需要一种更高效的方法来降低标注成本和时间开销。

Method: 本文形式化了自然语言处理中的主动测试框架，并对现有方法进行了广泛的基准测试，涵盖18个数据集、4种嵌入策略以及4种不同的自然语言处理任务，同时引入了一种自适应停止准则来自动确定最优标注样本数量。

Result: 实验结果表明，与完整测试集相比，最多可实现95%的标注量减少，同时性能估计的准确性差异保持在1%以内，但不同方法在不同数据特征和任务类型上的表现存在差异，没有单一方法在所有情况下都表现最佳。

Conclusion: 主动测试能够有效减少自然语言处理中的标注成本，提高测试效率，但其方法效果受数据特性和任务类型影响较大，未来应关注自适应和通用性更强的采样策略设计。

Abstract: Human annotation cost and time remain significant bottlenecks in Natural Language Processing (NLP), with test data annotation being particularly expensive due to the stringent requirement for low-error and high-quality labels necessary for reliable model evaluation. Traditional approaches require annotating entire test sets, leading to substantial resource requirements. Active Testing is a framework that selects the most informative test samples for annotation. Given a labeling budget, it aims to choose the subset that best estimates model performance while minimizing cost and human effort. In this work, we formalize Active Testing in NLP and we conduct an extensive benchmarking of existing approaches across 18 datasets and 4 embedding strategies spanning 4 different NLP tasks. The experiments show annotation reductions of up to 95%, with performance estimation accuracy difference from the full test set within 1%. Our analysis reveals variations in method effectiveness across different data characteristics and task types, with no single approach emerging as universally superior. Lastly, to address the limitation of requiring a predefined annotation budget in existing sample selection strategies, we introduce an adaptive stopping criterion that automatically determines the optimal number of samples.

</details>


### [262] [Riding Brainwaves in LLM Space: Understanding Activation Patterns Using Individual Neural Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21847)
*Ajan Subramanian,Sumukh Bettadapura,Rohan Sathish*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 冻结的语言模型深层中存在稳定的、个体特定的神经方向，可用于个性化脑电图（EEG）解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索消费级EEG设备普及背景下，语言模型是否能捕捉个体特有的神经反应模式。

Method: 使用ZuCo语料库中30名被试阅读自然句子时的词级EEG数据，为每个人训练独立的线性探针，将冻结的Qwen 2.5 7B模型隐藏状态映射到个体EEG功率，并与群体探针对比。

Result: 个体特异性探针在所有EEG特征上均优于群体探针；对于高γ波功率，相关系数rho达0.183，是群体探针（rho=0.020）的九倍；该信号在深层模型中集中（峰值在第24层），具有时间稳定性、不可跨人迁移性，并独立于共享的群体信号。结果在LLaMA 3.1 8B上一致且通过混淆变量控制。

Conclusion: 冻结语言模型的深层表征中包含稳定且个体特有的神经方向，为基于EEG的个性化提供了几何基础。

Abstract: Consumer-grade EEG is entering everyday devices, from earbuds to headbands, raising the question of whether language models can be adapted to individual neural responses. We test this by asking whether frozen LLM representations encode person-specific EEG signals, directions in activation space that predict one person's brain activity but not another's. Using word-level EEG from 30 participants reading naturalistic sentences (ZuCo corpus), we train a separate linear probe for each person, mapping hidden states from a frozen Qwen 2.5 7B to that individual's EEG power. Person-specific probes outperform a single population probe on every EEG feature tested; for high-gamma power, the person-specific probe achieves rho = 0.183, a ninefold improvement over the population probe (rho = 0.020, p < 10^-4). A negative control, fixation count, shows no person-specific advantage (p = 0.360); fixation count reflects word length and frequency rather than individual cognition. The individual directions are temporally stable (split-half cosine = 0.824), non-transferable across people (self rho = 0.369 vs. other rho = 0.143, p < 10^-19), and distinct from the shared population signal: person-specific probes retain predictive power after the population component is removed. The person-specific signal concentrates in the model's deep layers, rising consistently with depth and peaking at Layer 24 of 28. The results are consistent across architectures (LLaMA 3.1 8B) and survive word-level confound controls. Frozen language models contain stable, person-specific neural directions in their deep layers, providing a geometric foundation for EEG-driven personalization.

</details>


### [263] [Ara-Best-RQ: Multi Dialectal Arabic SSL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21900)
*Haroun Elleuch,Ryan Whetten,Salima Mdhaffar,Yannick Estève,Fethi Bougares*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了Ara-BEST-RQ模型家族，专用于多方言阿拉伯语语音处理，在方言识别和自动语音识别任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在处理多方言阿拉伯语语音时性能不足，缺乏专门针对阿拉伯语方言的自监督学习模型。

Method: 利用5640小时的Creative Commons语音数据及公开数据集，训练基于conformer的BEST-RQ模型，参数规模高达6亿。

Result: 在方言识别任务上达到最先进水平，且参数量少于竞争模型；相较于多语言或单语非阿拉伯语模型，针对阿拉伯语方言的预训练显著提升下游任务性能。

Conclusion: Ara-BEST-RQ通过针对性预训练有效提升了阿拉伯语语音处理性能，所有模型、代码和数据将公开以促进后续研究。

Abstract: We present Ara-BEST-RQ, a family of self-supervised learning (SSL) models specifically designed for multi-dialectal Arabic speech processing. Leveraging 5,640 hours of crawled Creative Commons speech and combining it with publicly available datasets, we pre-train conformer-based BEST-RQ models up to 600M parameters. Our models are evaluated on dialect identification (DID) and automatic speech recognition (ASR) tasks, achieving state-of-the-art performance on the former while using fewer parameters than competing models. We demonstrate that family-targeted pre-training on Arabic dialects significantly improves downstream performance compared to multilingual or monolingual models trained on non-Arabic data. All models, code, and pre-processed datasets will be publicly released to support reproducibility and further research in Arabic speech technologies.

</details>


### [264] [SLURP-TN : Resource for Tunisian Dialect Spoken Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21940)
*Haroun Elleuch,Salima Mdhaffar,Yannick Estève,Fethi Bougares*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SLURP-TN，一个用于突尼斯方言口语语言理解（SLU）的新数据集，包含4165个句子和约5小时的音频，并提供了基于该数据集训练的自动语音识别和SLU基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于缺乏资源，大多数低资源语言未能受益于SLU技术的进步。本文旨在通过构建突尼斯方言的SLU数据集来缓解这一问题。

Method: 通过让55名母语者朗读来自SLURP六个领域的句子，收集并手动翻译生成SLURP-TN数据集，并开发了多个基于该数据集的ASR和SLU模型。

Result: 成功构建了包含4165个句子、约5小时音频的突尼斯方言SLU数据集SLURP-TN，并发布了相应的基线模型。

Conclusion: SLURP-TN为突尼斯方言的SLU研究提供了重要资源，有助于推动低资源语言在任务导向对话系统中的应用。

Abstract: Spoken Language Understanding (SLU) aims to extract the semantic information from the speech utterance of user queries. It is a core component in a task-oriented dialogue system. With the spectacular progress of deep neural network models and the evolution of pre-trained language models, SLU has obtained significant breakthroughs. However, only a few high-resource languages have taken advantage of this progress due to the absence of SLU resources. In this paper, we seek to mitigate this obstacle by introducing SLURP-TN. This dataset was created by recording 55 native speakers uttering sentences in Tunisian dialect, manually translated from six SLURP domains. The result is an SLU Tunisian dialect dataset that comprises 4165 sentences recorded into around 5 hours of acoustic material. We also develop a number of Automatic Speech Recognition and SLU models exploiting SLUTP-TN. The Dataset and baseline models are available at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Elyadata/SLURP-TN.

</details>


### [265] [Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Medical Text Summarization: A Comparative Study of Lora, Prompt Tuning, and Full Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21970)
*Ulugbek Shernazarov,Rostislav Svitsov,Bin Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了LoRA、Prompt Tuning和全量微调在医疗文本摘要任务中的表现，发现LoRA以极少量可训练参数显著优于全量微调，表明低秩约束具有正则化优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少大模型在特定领域（如医疗）微调时的计算资源需求。

Method: 在Flan-T5模型家族上使用PubMed数据集，对比LoRA、Prompt Tuning和全量微调三种方法，并进行敏感性分析。

Result: LoRA在仅0.6%可训练参数下达到43.52 +/- 0.18 ROUGE-1，优于全量微调的40.67 +/- 0.21。

Conclusion: 低秩约束不仅节省资源，还提供有益的正则化效果，挑战了必须全参数更新的传统假设。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models for domain-specific tasks such as medical text summarization demands substantial computational resources. Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods offer promising alternatives by updating only a small fraction of parameters. This paper compares three adaptation approaches-Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), Prompt Tuning, and Full Fine-Tuning-across the Flan-T5 model family on the PubMed medical summarization dataset. Through experiments with multiple random seeds, we demonstrate that LoRA consistently outperforms full fine-tuning, achieving 43.52 +/- 0.18 ROUGE-1 on Flan-T5-Large with only 0.6% trainable parameters compared to 40.67 +/- 0.21 for full fine-tuning. Sensitivity analyses examine the impact of LoRA rank and prompt token count. Our findings suggest the low-rank constraint provides beneficial regularization, challenging assumptions about the necessity of full parameter updates. Code is available at https://github.com/eracoding/llm-medical-summarization

</details>


### [266] [Retrieving Climate Change Disinformation by Narrative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22015)
*Max Upravitelev,Veronika Solopova,Charlott Jakob,Premtim Sahitaj,Vera Schmitt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将气候虚假信息叙事检测重构为检索任务，利用SpecFi框架生成假设文档以匹配抽象叙述与具体文本，并通过社区摘要提升检索效果，能够有效应对新兴叙事并保持对高变异度叙事的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于固定分类体系的气候虚假信息检测方法难以应对不断出现的新叙事，因此需要一种无需预定义标签集且能适应新叙事的灵活方法。

Method: 将叙事检测视为检索任务，使用查询表示核心信息；利用图聚类得到的社区摘要作为少样本示例，通过SpecFi框架生成假想文档；引入基于嵌入的‘叙事方差’衡量难度，并评估不同方法在各类叙事上的表现。

Result: SpecFi在无访问标签的情况下于CARDS数据集上达到0.505的MAP；标准检索方法在高方差叙事中性能显著下降（BM25损失63.4% MAP），而SpecFi-CS仅损失32.7%；发现无监督社区摘要接近专家设计的分类体系。

Conclusion: 将叙事检测转为检索任务并结合社区引导的生成方法是一种有效且灵活的策略，既能发现潜在叙事结构，又对新兴和多样化叙事具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Detecting climate disinformation narratives typically relies on fixed taxonomies, which do not accommodate emerging narratives. Thus, we re-frame narrative detection as a retrieval task: given a narrative's core message as a query, rank texts from a corpus by alignment with that narrative. This formulation requires no predefined label set and can accommodate emerging narratives. We repurpose three climate disinformation datasets (CARDS, Climate Obstruction, climate change subset of PolyNarrative) for retrieval evaluation and propose SpecFi, a framework that generates hypothetical documents to bridge the gap between abstract narrative descriptions and their concrete textual instantiations. SpecFi uses community summaries from graph-based community detection as few-shot examples for generation, achieving a MAP of 0.505 on CARDS without access to narrative labels. We further introduce narrative variance, an embedding-based difficulty metric, and show via partial correlation analysis that standard retrieval degrades on high-variance narratives (BM25 loses 63.4% of MAP), while SpecFi-CS remains robust (32.7% loss). Our analysis also reveals that unsupervised community summaries converge on descriptions close to expert-crafted taxonomies, suggesting that graph-based methods can surface narrative structure from unlabeled text.

</details>


### [267] [Dual-Space Knowledge Distillation with Key-Query Matching for Large Language Models with Vocabulary Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22056)
*Stella Eva Tsiapali,Cong-Thanh Do,Kate Knill*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于生成对抗学习的新型跨模型注意力知识蒸馏方法DSKD-CMA-GA，用于改善不同分词器间大语言模型知识迁移的效果，尤其在分布外数据上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DSKD-CMA方法在不同分词器的大模型间进行知识蒸馏时存在键值分布不匹配的问题，且机制不够透明，限制了性能提升。

Method: 通过人工对齐探针和热图可视化分析DSKD-CMA的注意力机制，并引入生成对抗学习策略，提出DSKD-CMA-GA以对齐跨模型的键和查询分布。

Result: 实验显示DSKD-CMA-GA在ROUGE-L指标上取得一致提升，尤其在分布外数据上平均提升0.37，缩小了跨分词与同分词蒸馏之间的差距。

Conclusion: DSKD-CMA-GA有效缓解了跨模型注意力中分布不匹配的问题，提升了知识蒸馏在异构分词器设置下的生成质量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across language tasks, but are costly to deploy due to their size and resource demands. Knowledge Distillation (KD) addresses this by training smaller Student models to mimic larger Teacher models, improving efficiency without significant performance loss. Dual-Space Knowledge Distillation with Cross-Model Attention (DSKD-CMA) has emerged as a SOTA method for KD between LLMs with distinct tokenizers, yet its internal workings remain largely opaque. In this work, we systematically analyse the attention mechanism of DSKD-CMA through manual token alignment probing and heatmap visualisations, revealing both strengths and limitations. Building on this, we introduce a novel method, DSKD-CMA-GA, based on Generative Adversarial (GA) learning, to address the mismatched distributions between the keys and queries computed from distinct models. Experiments show modest but consistent ROUGE-L gains in text generation quality, particularly on out-of-distribution data (+0.37 on average), narrowing the gap between cross- and same-tokenizer KD.

</details>


### [268] [Autoregressive vs. Masked Diffusion Language Models: A Controlled Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22075)
*Caio Vicentino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对自回归（AR）和掩码扩散（MDLM）语言模型进行了受控的实证比较，发现两者在相同训练条件下具有相近的吞吐量，但收敛行为和生成特性显著不同：AR收敛更快但易过拟合且输出重复，MDLM生成更多样化文本但略有语法问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在隔离生成范式这一变量，比较自回归与掩码扩散语言模型在相同数据、计算和硬件条件下的性能差异，以理解其各自优劣。

Method: 在完全相同的训练设置下（50M TinyStories数据、20,000步、batch size 32、H100 GPU），训练AR和MDLM模型，并对比其训练效率、收敛速度、过拟合情况以及生成文本的多样性与流畅性。

Result: 1. 两种模型训练吞吐量相近，MDLM仅多耗时4.7%；2. AR在14,000步即开始过拟合，MDLM在20,000步仍持续提升；3. 生成上AR更流畅但高度重复（99.8%样本以相同词开头），MDLM更多样（93.4%唯一五词开头，更高Distinct-n，更低Self-BLEU），但偶有语法错误。

Conclusion: AR和MDLM各有权衡：AR适合追求高流畅性的场景，而MDLM更适合需要结构多样性的任务，二者存在明显的多样性-流畅性权衡，且最优计算分配策略可能不同。

Abstract: We present a controlled empirical comparison between autoregressive (AR) and masked diffusion (MDLM) language models. Both models are trained on identical data (50M tokens from TinyStories), identical compute budget (20,000 steps, batch size 32, sequence length 512), and identical hardware (NVIDIA H100 80GB), isolating the generation paradigm as the sole variable. We report three findings. First, both paradigms achieve comparable training throughput (~50K tokens/second), with MDLM requiring only 4.7% more wall-clock time. Second, AR converges faster and begins overfitting by step 14,000, while MDLM converges more slowly and is still improving at step 20,000, suggesting different compute-optimal training regimes. Third, quantitative diversity analysis over 1,000 generated samples reveals a structural diversity-fluency trade-off: AR produces fluent but repetitive outputs (99.8% begin with the same word), while MDLM generates more diverse narratives (93.4% unique 5-word openings, higher Distinct-n, lower Self-BLEU), at the cost of occasional grammatical inconsistencies. All code, trained checkpoints, and data pipelines are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [269] [Multiperspectivity as a Resource for Narrative Similarity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22103)
*Max Upravitelev,Veronika Solopova,Jing Yang,Charlott Jakob,Premtim Sahitaj,Ariana Sahitaj,Vera Schmitt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过构建31个LLM角色的集成模型来处理叙事相似性预测中的多视角解释问题，在SemEval-2026 Task 4数据集上取得了0.705的准确率，发现专家型角色个体表现较差但误差相关性低，提升了整体性能，并揭示性别相关解读词汇与准确率呈负相关，强调评估框架需容纳解释多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叙事相似性预测本质上具有多重解释性，传统语义评估基准仅编码单一真实标签，难以反映不同但同样合理的解读，因此需要一种能纳入多视角判断的预测方法。

Method: 构建包含31个LLM角色的集成模型，涵盖专业解释框架实践者和直觉型非专业角色；在SemEval-2026 Task 4数据集上进行实验，采用多数投票机制融合预测结果，并分析不同角色的表现及误差模式。

Result: 系统在SemEval-2026 Task 4上达到0.705的准确率，准确率随集成规模增加而提升；专业角色个体表现较差但误差更不相关，带来更大的集成增益；错误分析显示性别导向的解读词汇与准确率呈一致负相关。

Conclusion: 将解释多样性纳入预测系统是可行且有益的，集成不同LLM角色可提升性能并揭示当前评估标准忽略的有效解读维度，呼吁建立支持解释多元性的新评估框架。

Abstract: Predicting narrative similarity can be understood as an inherently interpretive task: different, equally valid readings of the same text can produce divergent interpretations and thus different similarity judgments, posing a fundamental challenge for semantic evaluation benchmarks that encode a single ground truth. Rather than treating this multiperspectivity as a challenge to overcome, we propose to incorporate it in the decision making process of predictive systems. To explore this strategy, we created an ensemble of 31 LLM personas. These range from practitioners following interpretive frameworks to more intuitive, lay-style characters. Our experiments were conducted on the SemEval-2026 Task 4 dataset, where the system achieved an accuracy score of 0.705. Accuracy improves with ensemble size, consistent with Condorcet Jury Theorem-like dynamics under weakened independence. Practitioner personas perform worse individually but produce less correlated errors, yielding larger ensemble gains under majority voting. Our error analysis reveals a consistent negative association between gender-focused interpretive vocabulary and accuracy across all persona categories, suggesting either attention to dimensions not relevant for the benchmark or valid interpretations absent from the ground truth. This finding underscores the need for evaluation frameworks that account for interpretive plurality.

</details>


### [270] [The Semantic Ladder: A Framework for Progressive Formalization of Natural Language Content for Knowledge Graphs and AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22136)
*Lars Vogt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了“语义阶梯”框架，通过分层逐步形式化数据和知识，弥合自然语言与形式化语义模型之间的鸿沟，支持语义连续性和可追溯性，构建可扩展、互操作且适合AI的知识基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合自然语言表达与形式化语义模型之间的根本差异，解决在数据录入时即需完全语义形式化的难题。

Method: 提出“语义阶梯”架构框架，基于模块化语义单元，将表示按语义显式程度分层，支持从自然语言到本体乃至高阶逻辑模型的渐进转化，并保持语义连续与可追溯。

Result: 实现了数据的渐进式语义增强、结构化陈述与逻辑建模，降低了语义解析负担，整合了自然语言、结构化模型和向量嵌入等异构表示。

Conclusion: 语义阶梯为构建可扩展、互操作且兼容人工智能的数据与知识基础设施提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Semantic data and knowledge infrastructures must reconcile two fundamentally different forms of representation: natural language, in which most knowledge is created and communicated, and formal semantic models, which enable machine-actionable integration, interoperability, and reasoning. Bridging this gap remains a central challenge, particularly when full semantic formalization is required at the point of data entry. Here, we introduce the Semantic Ladder, an architectural framework that enables the progressive formalization of data and knowledge. Building on the concept of modular semantic units as identifiable carriers of meaning, the framework organizes representations across levels of increasing semantic explicitness, ranging from natural language text snippets to ontology-based and higher-order logical models. Transformations between levels support semantic enrichment, statement structuring, and logical modelling while preserving semantic continuity and traceability. This approach enables the incremental construction of semantic knowledge spaces, reduces the semantic parsing burden, and supports the integration of heterogeneous representations, including natural language, structured semantic models, and vector-based embeddings. The Semantic Ladder thereby provides a foundation for scalable, interoperable, and AI-ready data and knowledge infrastructures.

</details>


### [271] [Enhancing Document-Level Machine Translation via Filtered Synthetic Corpora and Two-Stage LLM Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22186)
*Ireh Kim,Tesia Sker,Chanwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段微调策略，利用大语言模型增强的文档级数据来改进机器翻译，特别是在处理跨句子一致性方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在文档级翻译任务中具有潜力，但由于缺乏高质量的文档级平行数据和生成过程中的幻觉与遗漏问题，其应用受到限制。

Method: 通过将摘要数据转换为文档级平行数据并使用多个指标过滤以提高数据质量，然后采用两阶段微调策略：先在句子级MT资源上微调，再在过滤后的文档级语料上微调。

Result: 该方法有效缓解了大语言模型在文档级翻译中的数据稀缺和生成质量问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段微调策略结合LLM增强数据，提升了文档级机器翻译的质量与连贯性。

Abstract: In Machine Translation, Large Language Models (LLMs) have generally underperformed compared to conventional encoder-decoder systems and thus see limited adoption. However, LLMs excel at modeling contextual information, making them a natural fit for document-level translation tasks where coherence across sentences is crucial. Despite this potential, document-level MT with LLMs faces two key challenges: (1) the scarcity of large-scale, high-quality document-level parallel data; and (2) the propensity of LLMs to introduce hallucinations and omissions during generation. To address these challenges, we propose a two-stage fine-tuning strategy leveraging LLM-augmented document-level data. First, we augment data by converting summarization data into document-level parallel data using a LLM, and then filter it using multiple metrics, leveraging sacreBLEU, COMET, and LaBSE-based cosine similarity-to improve data quality. Finally, we employ a two-stage fine-tuning strategy: first fine-tuning on the abundant sentence-level MT resources, and then on the filtered document-level corpus.

</details>


### [272] [Gumbel Distillation for Parallel Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22216)
*Chi Zhang,Xixi Hu,Bo Liu,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Gumbel Distillation是一种新的蒸馏技术，通过Gumbel-Max技巧帮助并行解码语言模型更好地学习自回归教师模型的联合分布，显著提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非自回归（非AR）模型虽然支持并行解码、加快生成速度，但通常在建模token序列的复杂联合分布上表现较差，导致生成质量下降。本文旨在缩小非AR模型与AR模型在生成性能上的差距。

Method: 提出Gumbel Distillation方法，利用Gumbel-Max技巧构建从隐式Gumbel噪声空间到自回归教师模型输出token的确定性映射，使并行解码模型能有效学习该分布。该方法不依赖特定模型结构，可兼容多种并行架构（如MDLM和BD3-LM）。

Result: 在LM1B和OpenWebText数据集上的实验表明，使用Gumbel Distillation的并行语言模型在MAUVE得分上提升了30.0%，生成困惑度改善了10.5%（相较于在OpenWebText上训练的MDLM）。

Conclusion: Gumbel Distillation是一种高效且模型无关的蒸馏方法，能够显著提升并行解码语言模型的生成质量，缩小其与自回归模型之间的性能差距。

Abstract: The slow, sequential nature of autoregressive (AR) language models has driven the adoption of parallel decoding methods. However, these non-AR models often sacrifice generation quality as they struggle to model the complex joint distribution of token sequences. To narrow this performance gap, we introduce Gumbel Distillation, a novel distillation technique that enables parallel decoders to learn this distribution effectively. Our method leverages the Gumbel-Max trick to create a deterministic mapping from a latent Gumbel noise space to the output tokens of a high-performing AR teacher. As a model-agnostic technique, Gumbel Distillation seamlessly integrates with diverse parallel decoding architectures, including MDLM and BD3-LM. Experiments on LM1B and OpenWebText show that Gumbel Distillation substantially improves the generation quality of parallel language models, achieving a 30.0% improvement in MAUVE score and 10.5% in generative perplexity over MDLM trained on OpenWebText dataset. Code available at https://github.com/hxixixh/gumbel-distill.

</details>


### [273] [Adapting Self-Supervised Speech Representations for Cross-lingual Dysarthria Detection in Parkinson's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22225)
*Abner Hernandez,Eunjung Yeo,Kwanghee Choi,Chin-Jou Li,Zhengjun Yue,Rohan Kumar Das,Jan Rusz,Mathew Magimai Doss,Juan Rafael Orozco-Arroyave,Tomás Arias-Vergara,Andreas Maier,Elmar Nöth,David R. Mortensen,David Harwath,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种表示层语言迁移方法（LS），用于在跨语言和多语言环境下提升帕金森病言语障碍的检测性能，通过减少嵌入空间中的语言依赖性结构来改善模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于构音障碍语音数据有限，跨语言检测面临挑战，尤其是语音表征中包含的语言相关结构可能干扰检测效果。

Method: 提出基于质心向量自适应的表示层语言迁移（LS）方法，利用健康人语音估计源语言与目标语言之间的分布对齐，调整自监督语音表示。

Result: 在捷克语、德语和西班牙语的帕金森病语音数据集上验证，LS显著提升了跨语言设置下的敏感性和F1分数，在多语言设置下也有稳定增益；分析表明LS有效减少了嵌入空间中的语言身份信息。

Conclusion: LS能够有效削弱语音表示中的语言依赖性，增强跨语言场景下构音障碍检测的泛化能力，为低资源语言环境下的临床应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The limited availability of dysarthric speech data makes cross-lingual detection an important but challenging problem. A key difficulty is that speech representations often encode language-dependent structure that can confound dysarthria detection. We propose a representation-level language shift (LS) that aligns source-language self-supervised speech representations with the target-language distribution using centroid-based vector adaptation estimated from healthy-control speech. We evaluate the approach on oral DDK recordings from Parkinson's disease speech datasets in Czech, German, and Spanish under both cross-lingual and multilingual settings. LS substantially improves sensitivity and F1 in cross-lingual settings, while yielding smaller but consistent gains in multilingual settings. Representation analysis further shows that LS reduces language identity in the embedding space, supporting the interpretation that LS removes language-dependent structure.

</details>


### [274] [MemDLM: Memory-Enhanced DLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22241)
*Zehua Pei,Hui-Ling Zhen,Weizhe Lin,Sinno Jialin Pan,Yunhe Wang,Mingxuan Yuan,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemDLM通过引入参数化记忆机制，利用双层优化将去噪过程模拟融入训练，缩小了扩散语言模型的训练-推理差异，并在推理时进一步激活内存以提升长上下文理解和检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型存在训练与推理阶段不匹配的问题：训练时使用单步掩码预测，而推理时采用多步渐进去噪。这种不一致影响模型性能，因此需要一种方法来缩小该差距。

Method: 提出MemDLM，采用双层优化框架：内层更新一组快速权重形成参数化记忆，记录每个样本的局部去噪轨迹；外层基于该记忆更新基础模型。此机制将在训练中模拟多步去噪过程，并可在推理时重新启用内层进行自适应调整。

Result: MemDLM实现了更快的收敛速度和更低的训练损失；在推理时启用参数化记忆可提升长上下文理解能力，并在‘大海捞针’等检索任务中展现出 emergent 的权重内检索机制，缓解了词元级注意力瓶颈。

Conclusion: MemDLM有效缩小了扩散语言模型的训练-推理差距，通过参数化记忆增强了模型表达能力和推理适应性，为扩散语言模型的设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) offer attractive advantages over Auto-Regressive (AR) models, such as full-attention parallel decoding and flexible generation. However, they suffer from a notable train-inference mismatch: DLMs are trained with a static, single-step masked prediction objective, but deployed through a multi-step progressive denoising trajectory. We propose MemDLM (Memory-Enhanced DLM), which narrows this gap by embedding a simulated denoising process into training via Bi-level Optimization. An inner loop updates a set of fast weights, forming a Parametric Memory that captures the local trajectory experience of each sample, while an outer loop updates the base model conditioned on this memory. By offloading memorization pressure from token representations to parameters, MemDLM yields faster convergence and lower training loss. Moreover, the inner loop can be re-enabled at inference time as an adaptation step, yielding additional gains on long-context understanding. We find that, when activated at inference time, this Parametric Memory acts as an emergent in-weight retrieval mechanism, helping MemDLM further reduce token-level attention bottlenecks on challenging Needle-in-a-Haystack retrieval tasks. Code: https://github.com/JarvisPei/MemDLM.

</details>


### [275] [Greater accessibility can amplify discrimination in generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22260)
*Carolin Holtermann,Minh Duc Bui,Kaitlyn Zhou,Valentin Hofmann,Katharina von der Wense,Anne Lauscher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 语音交互虽然提升了AI的可访问性，但会引入基于声音中性别特征的新偏见机制，导致系统性性别歧视，且用户对隐式属性推断普遍抵触；研究发现通过音调调节等手段可能缓解此类问题，提示公平性与可访问性需协同考虑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语音大模型在提升可及性的同时是否引入新的社会偏见，特别是基于语音中性别线索的歧视问题，并评估用户对隐式属性推断的接受度。

Method: 分析音频大语言模型在不同性别声音输入下的输出倾向，检测其在形容词和职业推荐上的性别刻板化偏差，并与文本输入结果对比；结合千人调查评估用户对属性推断的态度；实验测试音调操控对偏见缓解的效果。

Result: 发现语音大模型存在系统性性别歧视，仅凭说话者声音就使输出偏向性别刻板印象，且偏见程度超过文本模式；调查显示不常使用聊天机器人者最反对未披露的属性推断；音调调节可调控性别偏见输出。

Conclusion: 语音接口不仅将文本模型扩展到新模态，还引入了与副语言特征相关的独特偏见机制；提升可访问性的同时可能加剧不公平，因此必须同时考虑公平性与可访问性的设计。

Abstract: Hundreds of millions of people rely on large language models (LLMs) for education, work, and even healthcare. Yet these models are known to reproduce and amplify social biases present in their training data. Moreover, text-based interfaces remain a barrier for many, for example, users with limited literacy, motor impairments, or mobile-only devices. Voice interaction promises to expand accessibility, but unlike text, speech carries identity cues that users cannot easily mask, raising concerns about whether accessibility gains may come at the cost of equitable treatment. Here we show that audio-enabled LLMs exhibit systematic gender discrimination, shifting responses toward gender-stereotyped adjectives and occupations solely on the basis of speaker voice, and amplifying bias beyond that observed in text-based interaction. Thus, voice interfaces do not merely extend text models to a new modality but introduce distinct bias mechanisms tied to paralinguistic cues. Complementary survey evidence ($n=1,000$) shows that infrequent chatbot users are most hesitant to undisclosed attribute inference and most likely to disengage when such practices are revealed. To demonstrate a potential mitigation strategy, we show that pitch manipulation can systematically regulate gender-discriminatory outputs. Overall, our findings reveal a critical tension in AI development: efforts to expand accessibility through voice interfaces simultaneously create new pathways for discrimination, demanding that fairness and accessibility be addressed in tandem.

</details>


### [276] [TiCo: Time-Controllable Training for Spoken Dialogue Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22267)
*Kai-Wei Chang,Wei-Chih Chen,En-Pei Hu,Hung-yi Lee,James Glass*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TiCo是一种简单的训练后方法，通过引入口语时间标记（STM）使对话模型具备时间感知能力，从而生成符合指定时长要求的响应，且保持高质量输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有口语对话模型缺乏时间意识，难以遵循与响应时长相关的指令，限制了在语音助手等实际应用中的交互质量。

Method: 提出TiCo方法，利用自生成数据和强化学习，在生成过程中引入口语时间标记（如<10.6秒>），使模型能估计已用时间和调整剩余内容以满足目标时长。

Result: 实验表明，TiCo显著提升了模型对时长约束的遵循能力，同时保持了响应的自然性和质量。

Conclusion: TiCo为现有口语对话模型提供了一种高效、低成本的时间控制增强方案，适用于需要可控响应时长的实际应用场景。

Abstract: We propose TiCo, a simple post-training method for enabling spoken dialogue models (SDMs) to follow time-constrained instructions and generate responses with controllable duration. This capability is valuable for real-world spoken language systems such as voice assistants and interactive agents, where controlling response duration can improve interaction quality. However, despite their strong ability to generate natural spoken responses, existing models lack time awareness and struggle to follow duration-related instructions (e.g., "Please generate a response lasting about 15 seconds"). Through an empirical evaluation of both open-source and commercial SDMs, we show that they frequently fail to satisfy such time-control requirements. TiCo addresses this limitation by enabling models to estimate elapsed speaking time during generation through Spoken Time Markers (STM) (e.g., <10.6 seconds>). These markers help the model maintain awareness of time and adjust the remaining content to meet the target duration. TiCo is simple and efficient: it requires only a small amount of data and no additional question-answer pairs, relying instead on self-generation and reinforcement learning. Experimental results show that TiCo significantly improves adherence to duration constraints while preserving response quality.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [277] [Fusing Driver Perceived and Physical Risk for Safety Critical Scenario Screening in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20232)
*Chen Xiong,Ziwen Wang,Deqi Wang,Cheng Wang,Yiyang Chen,He Zhang,Chao Gou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于驾驶员风险融合的自动驾驶危险场景筛选方法，通过改进的驾驶员风险场和动态成本模型生成高质量风险监督信号，实现高效的场景级风险评分预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶安全关键场景筛选依赖人工风险标注和昂贵的逐帧风险评估，效率低且风险量化不准确。

Method: 结合改进的驾驶员风险场与动态成本模型生成风险监督信号，推理时通过前向传播直接预测场景级风险分数，并设计风险轨迹交叉注意力解码器联合解码风险与轨迹。

Result: 在INTERACTION和FLUID数据集上实验表明，该方法产生更平滑、更具区分性的风险估计，在FLUID上AUC达到0.792，AP达到0.825，分别比PODAR提升9.1%和5.1%。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效支持大规模风险标注与危险场景筛选，显著提升效率与风险量化质量。

Abstract: Autonomous driving testing increasingly relies on mining safety critical scenarios from large scale naturalistic driving data, yet existing screening pipelines still depend on manual risk annotation and expensive frame by frame risk evaluation, resulting in low efficiency and weakly grounded risk quantification. To address this issue, we propose a driver risk fusion based hazardous scenario screening method for autonomous driving. During training, the method combines an improved Driver Risk Field with a dynamic cost model to generate high quality risk supervision signals, while during inference it directly predicts scenario level risk scores through fast forward passes, avoiding per frame risk computation and enabling efficient large scale ranking and retrieval. The improved Driver Risk Field introduces a new risk height function and a speed adaptive look ahead mechanism, and the dynamic cost model integrates kinetic energy, oriented bounding box constraints, and Gaussian kernel diffusion smoothing for more accurate interaction modeling. We further design a risk trajectory cross attention decoder to jointly decode risk and trajectories. Experiments on the INTERACTION and FLUID datasets show that the proposed method produces smoother and more discriminative risk estimates. On FLUID, it achieves an AUC of 0.792 and an AP of 0.825, outperforming PODAR by 9.1 percent and 5.1 percent, respectively, demonstrating its effectiveness for scalable risk labeling and hazardous scenario screening.

</details>


### [278] [EnergyAction: Unimanual to Bimanual Composition with Energy-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20236)
*Mingchen Song,Xiang Deng,Jie Wei,Dongmei Jiang,Liqiang Nie,Weili Guan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为EnergyAction的新框架，通过能量基模型（EBM）将单臂操作策略组合式迁移至双臂操作任务中，在缺乏双臂数据的情况下实现了高效且协调的双臂动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于缺乏双臂演示数据以及双臂动作协调的复杂性，现有方法难以有效扩展单臂操作策略到双臂场景，且无法充分利用预训练的单臂策略。

Method: 将单臂策略建模为能量基模型（EBM），利用其可组合性融合左右臂动作；引入基于能量的时空协调机制，通过能量约束保证动作的时间连贯性和空间可行性；设计两种能量感知去噪策略，根据动作质量动态调整去噪步长。

Result: 实验结果表明，EnergyAction在极少双臂数据的情况下，在仿真和真实世界任务中均能有效迁移单臂知识并实现优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: EnergyAction通过EBM实现了从单臂到双臂操作的高效策略迁移，解决了数据稀缺和动作协调难题，为多臂机器人控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in unimanual manipulation policies have achieved remarkable success across diverse robotic tasks through abundant training data and well-established model architectures. However, extending these capabilities to bimanual manipulation remains challenging due to the lack of bimanual demonstration data and the complexity of coordinating dual-arm actions. Existing approaches either rely on extensive bimanual datasets or fail to effectively leverage pre-trained unimanual policies. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{EnergyAction}, a novel framework that compositionally transfers unimanual manipulation policies to bimanual tasks through the Energy-Based Models (EBMs). Specifically, our method incorporates three key innovations. First, we model individual unimanual policies as EBMs and leverage their compositional properties to compose left and right arm actions, enabling the fusion of unimanual policies into a bimanual policy. Second, we introduce an energy-based temporal-spatial coordination mechanism through energy constraints, ensuring the generated bimanual actions are both temporal coherence and spatial feasibility. Third, we propose two different energy-aware denoising strategies that dynamically adapt denoising steps based on action quality assessment. These strategies ensure the generation of high-quality actions while maintaining superior computational efficiency compared to fixed-step denoising approaches. Experimental results demonstrate that EnergyAction effectively transfers unimanual knowledge to bimanual tasks, achieving superior performance on both simulated and real-world tasks with minimal bimanual data.

</details>


### [279] [Rheos: Modelling Continuous Motion Dynamics in Hierarchical 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20239)
*Iacopo Catalano,Francesco Verdoja,Javier Civera,Jorge Peña-Queralta,Julio A. Placed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Rheos框架，将连续的方向运动模型嵌入到层次化3D场景图的动态层中，提升了导航性能，并通过概率建模和高效在线更新策略在多分辨率下优于离散基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D场景图对动态的处理局限于个体智能体跟踪，而动态地图虽能建模群体运动但缺乏语义支撑且扩展性差。

Method: 提出Rheos框架，在3D场景图中引入动态层，每个节点使用半缠绕高斯混合模型表示多模态方向流；采用蓄水池采样、并行更新和基于BIC准则的组件选择实现在线学习。

Result: 在四种空间分辨率的行人模拟环境中，Rheos在连续和离散指标下均优于离散基线方法。

Conclusion: Rheos通过语义与动态的深度融合，实现了可扩展、具不确定性的环境动态建模，显著提升导航性能。

Abstract: 3D Scene Graphs (3DSGs) provide hierarchical, multi-resolution abstractions that encode the geometric and semantic structure of an environment, yet their treatment of dynamics remains limited to tracking individual agents. Maps of Dynamics (MoDs) complement this by modeling aggregate motion patterns, but rely on uniform grid discretizations that lack semantic grounding and scale poorly. We present Rheos, a framework that explicitly embeds continuous directional motion models into an additional dynamics layer of a hierarchical 3DSG that enhances the navigational properties of the graph. Each dynamics node maintains a semi-wrapped Gaussian mixture model that captures multimodal directional flow as a principled probability distribution with explicit uncertainty, replacing the discrete histograms used in prior work. To enable online operation, Rheos employs reservoir sampling for bounded-memory observation buffers, parallel per-cell model updates and a principled Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC) sweep that selects the optimal number of mixture components, reducing per-update initialization cost from quadratic to linear in the number of samples. Evaluated across four spatial resolutions in a simulated pedestrian environment, Rheos consistently outperforms the discrete baseline under continuous as well as unfavorable discrete metrics. We release our implementation as open source.

</details>


### [280] [TRGS-SLAM: IMU-Aided Gaussian Splatting SLAM for Blurry, Rolling Shutter, and Noisy Thermal Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20443)
*Spencer Carmichael,Katherine A. Skinner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D高斯点阵的热红外惯性SLAM系统TRGS-SLAM，能够有效应对非制冷热像仪在移动机器人应用中的运动模糊、卷帘快门失真和固定模式噪声等挑战，并实现了在高速运动和高噪声条件下的准确跟踪与热图像恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热像仪在黑暗、光照变化大或有雾尘烟等恶劣环境中具有优势，但非制冷微测辐射热计热像仪存在运动模糊、卷帘快门失真和固定模式噪声等问题，限制了其在SLAM中的应用。因此需要开发能克服这些退化效应的SLAM系统。

Method: 提出了TRGS-SLAM系统，采用模型感知的3D高斯点阵渲染方法，并引入B样条轨迹优化结合两阶段IMU损失、基于视角多样性的不透明度重置以及位姿漂移校正方案等创新技术。

Result: 系统在真实世界高速运动和高噪声热数据上表现出准确的跟踪能力，优于其他测试的SLAM方法，并通过离线优化实现了与需真值姿态的先前工作相当的热图像恢复效果。

Conclusion: TRGS-SLAM是首个能有效处理非制冷热像仪多种退化问题的3D高斯点阵SLAM系统，在复杂环境下展现出优越性能，为热红外SLAM提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Thermal cameras offer several advantages for simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) with mobile robots: they provide a passive, low-power solution to operating in darkness, are invariant to rapidly changing or high dynamic range illumination, and can see through fog, dust, and smoke. However, uncooled microbolometer thermal cameras, the only practical option in most robotics applications, suffer from significant motion blur, rolling shutter distortions, and fixed pattern noise. In this paper, we present TRGS-SLAM, a 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) based thermal inertial SLAM system uniquely capable of handling these degradations. To overcome the challenges of thermal data, we introduce a model-aware 3DGS rendering method and several general innovations to 3DGS SLAM, including B-spline trajectory optimization with a two-stage IMU loss, view-diversity-based opacity resetting, and pose drift correction schemes. Our system demonstrates accurate tracking on real-world, fast motion, and high-noise thermal data that causes all other tested SLAM methods to fail. Moreover, through offline refinement of our SLAM results, we demonstrate thermal image restoration competitive with prior work that required ground truth poses.

</details>


### [281] [High-Speed, All-Terrain Autonomy: Ensuring Safety at the Limits of Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20525)
*James R. Baxter,Bogdan I. Epureanu,Paramsothy Jayakumar,Tulga Ersal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于高速越野自动驾驶车辆的新型局部轨迹规划器，通过基于能量约束和并行GPGPU计算的模型预测控制方法，有效避免翻车并实现实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在粗糙地形上高速行驶时无法有效预测和防止翻车，且缺乏实时可行性。

Method: 开发了一种新的模型预测控制（MPC）框架，采用针对非平面地形的车辆动力学模型，并引入基于能量的约束来保障极端机动下的稳定性，利用GPGPU并行计算保证实时性。

Result: 在仿真和全尺寸实车实验中，新方法相比现有先进方法在多个挑战性场景下实现了更少的翻车事故和更高的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 该方法能安全实现越野环境下的高速极端机动，显著提升自主车辆在复杂地形中的行驶能力与安全性。

Abstract: A novel local trajectory planner, capable of controlling an autonomous off-road vehicle on rugged terrain at high-speed is presented. Autonomous vehicles are currently unable to safely operate off-road at high-speed, as current approaches either fail to predict and mitigate rollovers induced by rough terrain or are not real-time feasible. To address this challenge, a novel model predictive control (MPC) formulation is developed for local trajectory planning. A new dynamics model for off-road vehicles on rough, non-planar terrain is derived and used for prediction. Extreme mobility, including tire liftoff without rollover, is safely enabled through a new energy-based constraint. The formulation is analytically shown to mitigate rollover types ignored by many state-of-the-art methods, and real-time feasibility is achieved through parallelized GPGPU computation. The planner's ability to provide safe, extreme trajectories is studied through both simulated trials and full-scale physical experiments. The results demonstrate fewer rollovers and more successes compared to a state-of-the-art baseline across several challenging scenarios that push the vehicle to its mobility limits.

</details>


### [282] [Memory Over Maps: 3D Object Localization Without Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20530)
*Rui Zhou,Xander Yap,Jianwen Cao,Allison Lau,Boyang Sun,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需构建完整3D场景重建的无地图目标定位方法，仅通过存储带有位姿的RGB-D关键帧作为轻量级视觉记忆，并在查询时利用视觉-语言模型检索与重排序候选视图，结合深度反投影和多视角融合实现稀疏的按需3D估计。该方法显著降低了预处理成本和存储开销，在多个基准上实现了优异的物体目标导航性能，且无需任务特定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统目标定位方法依赖于显式的3D场景表示（如点云、体素网格），存在建图时间长、存储开销大和可扩展性差的问题；而当前视觉-语言模型的发展使得直接在2D观测上进行语义推理成为可能，因此作者探索是否必须进行完整的3D重建才能实现有效定位。

Method: 提出一种无地图管道：仅保存带有位姿的RGB-D关键帧作为视觉记忆；在查询时先检索候选视图，再用视觉-语言模型对其进行重排序，并通过深度反投影和多视图融合构建针对查询目标的稀疏、按需的3D位置估计。

Result: 相比基于重建的方法，本方法建图速度提升两个数量级以上，存储需求显著降低；在下游的物体目标导航任务中验证了定位结果的有效性，且无需任务特定训练即在多个基准上达到强性能。

Conclusion: 实验表明，基于图像的场景记忆上的直接推理可以有效替代密集3D重建，用于以物体为中心的机器人导航，证明了完整3D场景重建在某些场景下并非必要。

Abstract: Target localization is a prerequisite for embodied tasks such as navigation and manipulation. Conventional approaches rely on constructing explicit 3D scene representations to enable target localization, such as point clouds, voxel grids, or scene graphs. While effective, these pipelines incur substantial mapping time, storage overhead, and scalability limitations. Recent advances in vision-language models suggest that rich semantic reasoning can be performed directly on 2D observations, raising a fundamental question: is a complete 3D scene reconstruction necessary for object localization? In this work, we revisit object localization and propose a map-free pipeline that stores only posed RGB-D keyframes as a lightweight visual memory--without constructing any global 3D representation of the scene. At query time, our method retrieves candidate views, re-ranks them with a vision-language model, and constructs a sparse, on-demand 3D estimate of the queried target through depth backprojection and multi-view fusion. Compared to reconstruction-based pipelines, this design drastically reduces preprocessing cost, enabling scene indexing that is over two orders of magnitude faster to build while using substantially less storage. We further validate the localized targets on downstream object-goal navigation tasks. Despite requiring no task-specific training, our approach achieves strong performance across multiple benchmarks, demonstrating that direct reasoning over image-based scene memory can effectively replace dense 3D reconstruction for object-centric robot navigation. Project page: https://ruizhou-cn.github.io/memory-over-maps/

</details>


### [283] [LASER: Level-Based Asynchronous Scheduling and Execution Regime for Spatiotemporally Constrained Multi-Robot Timber Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20577)
*Zhenxiang Huang,Lior Skoury,Tim Stark,Aaron Wagner,Hans Jakob Wagner,Thomas Wortmann,Achim Menges*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LASER的框架，用于多机器人系统在大规模木质板材制造中的调度与执行，通过基于层级的异步调度机制实现任务并行与碰撞规避，并在实际全尺寸构件中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在如木结构建筑等大规模制造领域，需要多机器人协同作业以满足严格的时空约束（如避障和工艺时限），但现有方法难以兼顾可扩展性、并行性和鲁棒性，因此需要一种新的调度与执行框架。

Method: 提出LASER框架，将屏障机制引入基于约束规划（CP）的调度模型中，将任务划分为时空隔离的层级（levels），层内异步并行执行，层间通过屏障同步；针对大规模问题设计了两种专用算法：用于异构任务序列的迭代时间松弛法和用于同构任务的双层分解法以平衡负载。

Result: 在2.4m x 6m的全尺寸木板制造中，使用两机器人系统成功协调108个子程序和352颗螺丝，在严格胶合时间窗口下完成压螺钉胶接任务；计算实验表明，相比整体式方法，该方法在扩展性方面表现更优。

Conclusion: LASER框架通过层级化调度结构实现了复杂装配任务的安全、高效并行执行，具备良好的可扩展性和对时序不确定性的鲁棒性，适用于大型多机器人制造系统。

Abstract: Automating large-scale manufacturing in domains like timber construction requires multi-robot systems to manage tightly coupled spatiotemporal constraints, such as collision avoidance and process-driven deadlines. This paper introduces LASER (Level-based Asynchronous Scheduling and Execution Regime), a complete framework for scheduling and executing complex assembly tasks, demonstrated on a screw-press gluing application for timber slab manufacturing. Our central contribution is to integrate a barrier-based mechanism into a constraint programming (CP) scheduling formulation that partitions tasks into spatiotemporally disjoint sets, which we define as levels. This structure enables robots to execute tasks in parallel and asynchronously within a level, synchronizing only at level barriers, which guarantees collision-free operation by construction and provides robustness to timing uncertainties. To solve this formulation for large problems, we propose two specialized algorithms: an iterative temporal-relaxation approach for heterogeneous task sequences and a bi-level decomposition for homogeneous tasks that balances workload. We validate the LASER framework by fabricating a full-scale 2.4m x 6m timber slab with a two-robot system mounted on parallel linear tracks, successfully coordinating 108 subroutines and 352 screws under tight adhesive time windows. Computational studies show our method scales steadily with size compared to a monolithic approach.

</details>


### [284] [Unified Orbit-Attitude Estimation and Sensor Tasking Framework for Autonomous Cislunar Space Domain Awareness Using Multiplicative Unscented Kalman Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20579)
*Smriti Nandan Paul,Siwei Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向地月空间态势感知（SDA）的先进框架，包括观测器架构优化与基于互信息的传感器调度优化，并采用误差状态乘性无迹卡尔曼滤波进行状态估计，数值仿真验证了该方法在降低成本和传感器数量的同时提升了估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地月空间动力学具有强非线性与非开普勒特性，传统方法在不确定性传播与状态估计中精度不足，同时面临观测距离远、几何约束严、光照条件限制及传感器布局复杂等挑战，亟需高效的地月空间感知框架。

Method: 1) 基于真实成本模型，采用Tree of Parzen Estimators算法优化观测器架构；2) 在优化架构基础上，以互信息为指标进行离散时间点的传感器任务调度；3) 在任务更新间隔内，采用误差状态乘性无迹卡尔曼滤波进行高频率的轨道与姿态状态估计。

Result: 任务1结果显示，所提方法相比随机搜索基线方案，在更少传感器下显著降低了成本函数值；任务2结果表明，平动状态估计在较宽的目标-观测者比例范围内表现良好，而姿态估计对目标-观测者比例和调度频率更敏感，在目标多、调度少时出现明显旋转状态发散。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了传感资源、调度频率与状态估计性能之间的重要权衡关系，对构建可扩展的地月自主态势感知系统具有指导意义。

Abstract: The cislunar regime departs from near-Earth orbital behavior through strongly non-linear, non-Keplerian dynamics, which adversely affect the accuracy of uncertainty propagation and state estimation. Additional challenges arise from long-range observation requirements, restrictive sensor-target geometry and illumination conditions, the need to monitor an expansive cislunar volume, and the large design space associated with space/ground-based sensor placement. In response to these challenges, this work introduces an advanced framework for cislunar space domain awareness (SDA) encompassing two key tasks: (1) observer architecture optimization based on a realistic cost formulation that captures key performance trade-offs, solved using the Tree of Parzen Estimators algorithm, and (2) leveraging the resulting observer architecture, a mutual information-driven sensor tasking optimization is performed at discrete tasking intervals, while orbital and attitude state estimation is carried out at a finer temporal resolution between successive tasking updates using an error-state multiplicative unscented Kalman filter. Numerical simulations demonstrate that our approach in Task 1 yields observer architectures that achieve significantly lower values of the proposed cost function than baseline random-search solutions, while using fewer sensors. Task 2 results show that translational state estimation remains satisfactory over a wide range of target-to-observer count ratios, whereas attitude estimation is significantly more sensitive to target-to-observer ratios and tasking intervals, with increased rotational-state divergence observed for high target counts and infrequent tasking updates. These results highlight important trade-offs between sensing resources, tasking cadence, and achievable state estimation performance that influence the scalability of autonomous cislunar SDA.

</details>


### [285] [Towards Practical World Model-based Reinforcement Learning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20607)
*Zhilong Zhang,Haoxiang Ren,Yihao Sun,Yifei Sheng,Haonan Wang,Haoxin Lin,Zhichao Wu,Pierre-Luc Bacon,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLA-MBPO框架，通过统一多模态模型、多视角一致性机制和分块分支 rollout，提升视觉-语言-动作模型在模拟环境中的微调效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在真实环境中强化学习微调成本高、风险大的问题，同时克服基于世界模型训练中存在的像素级建模、多视角不一致和稀疏奖励下误差累积等挑战。

Method: 采用统一多模态模型（UMMs）实现数据高效的世界建模；设计交错视角解码机制以保证多视角一致性；引入chunk-level branched rollout减少误差累积。结合理论分析与仿真实验验证方法有效性。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界任务中，VLA-MBPO显著提升了策略性能和样本效率，表现出良好的鲁棒性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: VLA-MBPO为VLA模型的高效安全微调提供了可行方案，推动其在真实机器人部署中的应用。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models show strong generalization for robotic control, but finetuning them with reinforcement learning (RL) is constrained by the high cost and safety risks of real-world interaction. Training VLA models in interactive world models avoids these issues but introduces several challenges, including pixel-level world modeling, multi-view consistency, and compounding errors under sparse rewards. Building on recent advances across large multimodal models and model-based RL, we propose VLA-MBPO, a practical framework to tackle these problems in VLA finetuning. Our approach has three key design choices: (i) adapting unified multimodal models (UMMs) for data-efficient world modeling; (ii) an interleaved view decoding mechanism to enforce multi-view consistency; and (iii) chunk-level branched rollout to mitigate error compounding. Theoretical analysis and experiments across simulation and real-world tasks demonstrate that VLA-MBPO significantly improves policy performance and sample efficiency, underscoring its robustness and scalability for real-world robotic deployment.

</details>


### [286] [StageCraft: Execution Aware Mitigation of Distractor and Obstruction Failures in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20659)
*Kartikay Milind Pangaonkar,Prabin Rath,Omkar Patil,Nakul Gopalan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出StageCraft，一种无需训练的方法，利用大规模视觉语言模型的上下文推理能力，在执行任务前调整环境初始状态，以提升视觉语言动作模型在存在干扰物和物理遮挡时的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在面对执行时的干扰物和物理障碍时容易失败，现有的微调方法在新干扰环境下仍表现不佳，因此需要一种更鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 利用预训练的大规模视觉语言模型（VLM）进行上下文推理，输入策略 rollout 视频和成功标签，推断出初始状态中哪些物体需要被预先调整以避免失败；StageCraft作为即插即用模块，不修改原有策略，仅通过少量策略rollout即可工作。

Result: 在三个真实世界任务中实现了绝对40%的性能提升；在RLBench仿真中验证了其干预程度随策略强弱自适应，并且性能随上下文样本增多而提高。

Conclusion: StageCraft有效利用互联网规模预训练VLM的推理能力，显著提升了现有VLA策略在复杂、干扰环境下的鲁棒性和通用性，是一种高效、可扩展的前置环境调整框架。

Abstract: Large scale pre-training on text and image data along with diverse robot demonstrations has helped Vision Language Action models (VLAs) to generalize to novel tasks, objects and scenes. However, these models are still susceptible to failure in the presence of execution-time impediments such as distractors and physical obstructions in the robot's workspace. Existing policy improvement methods finetune base VLAs to improve generalization, yet they still struggle in unseen distractor settings. To address this problem, we investigate whether internet-scale pretraining of large vision-language models (VLMs) can be leveraged to reason about these impediments and mitigate policy failures. To this end, we propose StageCraft, a training-free approach to improve pretrained VLA policy performance by manipulating the environment's initial state using VLM-based in-context reasoning. StageCraft takes policy rollout videos and success labels as input and leverages VLM's reasoning ability to infer which objects in the initial state need to be manipulated to avoid anticipated execution failures. StageCraft is an extensible plug-and-play module that does not introduce additional constraints on the underlying policy, and only requires a few policy rollouts to work. We evaluate performance of state-of-the-art VLA models with StageCraft and show an absolute 40% performance improvement across three real world task domains involving diverse distractors and obstructions. Our simulation experiments in RLBench empirically show that StageCraft tailors its extent of intervention based on the strength of the underlying policy and improves its performance with more in-context samples. Videos of StageCraft in effect can be found at https://stagecraft-decorator.github.io/stagecraft/ .

</details>


### [287] [E-SocialNav: Efficient Socially Compliant Navigation with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20664)
*Ling Xiao,Daeun Song,Xuesu Xiao,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的面向社交合规导航的语言模型E-SocialNav，在较小数据集上训练后，其在生成符合社会规范的机器人导航行为方面优于零样本基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在机器人导航中的应用主要关注导航成功率，忽视了社交合规性，且大规模模型计算开销大，难以在资源受限的平台上实时部署。

Method: 采用两阶段训练流程：先进行监督微调，再进行直接偏好优化，以提升模型在文本语义相似性和动作准确性上的表现。

Result: E-SocialNav在文本级语义与人类标注的相似性以及动作准确率方面均优于GPT-4o和Claude等零样本大模型。

Conclusion: E-SocialNav是一种高效、轻量且具备良好社交合规性的语言模型，适用于资源受限的机器人平台上的实际部署。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) are increasingly applied to robotic navigation; however, existing benchmarks primarily emphasize navigation success rates while paying limited attention to social compliance. Moreover, relying on large-scale LMs can raise efficiency concerns, as their heavy computational overhead leads to slower response times and higher energy consumption, making them impractical for real-time deployment on resource-constrained robotic platforms. In this work, we evaluate the social compliance of GPT-4o and Claude in robotic navigation and propose E-SocialNav, an efficient LM designed for socially compliant navigation. Despite being trained on a relatively small dataset, E-SocialNav consistently outperforms zero-shot baselines in generating socially compliant behaviors. By employing a two-stage training pipeline consisting of supervised fine-tuning followed by direct preference optimization, E-SocialNav achieves strong performance in both text-level semantic similarity to human annotations and action accuracy. The source code is available at https://github.com/Dr-LingXiao/ESocialNav.

</details>


### [288] [ROI-Driven Foveated Attention for Unified Egocentric Representations in Vision-Language-Action Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20668)
*Xinhai Sun,Xiang Shi,Menglin Zou,Wenlong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于兴趣区域（ROI）的工程工作流，通过正向运动学将末端执行器姿态投影到外部相机视图中，生成以手为中心的ROI表示，实现跨具身形态的数据对齐与复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型在数据采集成本、跨具身对齐和互联网视觉数据向机器人控制迁移方面存在局限，需更高效的数据表示方法。

Method: 利用前向运动学将末端执行器姿态投影到单个外部相机视图，生成自我中心、几何定位的兴趣区域（ROI），并在原始图像上裁剪并保留上下文信息；构建可复现的处理流程，包括校准、同步、ROI生成等环节。

Result: 所提方法实现了具身对齐和视角归一化的数据表示，提升了接触关键区域的信息密度，并支持在不同机器人间的数据重用。

Conclusion: 自我中心ROI作为一种实用的数据抽象，有助于降低数据收集成本，促进大规模数据采集和跨具身形态的学习，弥合互联网级感知与机器人控制之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: The development of embodied AI systems is increasingly constrained by the availability and structure of physical interaction data. Despite recent advances in vision-language-action (VLA) models, current pipelines suffer from high data collection cost, limited cross-embodiment alignment, and poor transfer from internet-scale visual data to robot control.
  We propose a region-of-interest (ROI) driven engineering workflow that introduces an egocentric, geometry-grounded data representation. By projecting end-effector poses via forward kinematics (FK) into a single external camera, we derive movement-aligned hand-centric ROIs without requiring wrist-mounted cameras or multi-view systems. Unlike directly downsampling the full frame, ROI is cropped from the original image before resizing, preserving high local information density for contact-critical regions while retaining global context.
  We present a reproducible pipeline covering calibration, synchronization, ROI generation, deterministic boundary handling, and metadata governance. The resulting representation is embodiment-aligned and viewpoint-normalized, enabling data reuse across heterogeneous robots. We argue that egocentric ROI serves as a practical data abstraction for scalable collection and cross-embodiment learning, bridging internet-scale perception and robot-specific control.

</details>


### [289] [Enhancing Vision-Based Policies with Omni-View and Cross-Modality Knowledge Distillation for Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20679)
*Kai Li,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于知识蒸馏的方法，将全视角深度策略的知识迁移到轻量级单目策略中，提升移动机器人在场景迁移和导航中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决轻量型移动机器人在视觉策略中面临的场景迁移性差、计算资源受限和传感器成本高的三难问题。

Method: 采用知识蒸馏方法，让学生模型不仅模仿专家动作，还对齐全视角深度教师模型的潜在嵌入表示。

Result: 实验证明全视角深度输入提升了场景迁移和导航性能，所提蒸馏方法优于仅模仿动作的单目策略，且在真实环境中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效平衡了性能、计算开销与硬件成本，为轻量机器人提供了实用的视觉策略部署方案。

Abstract: Vision-based policies are widely applied in robotics for tasks such as manipulation and locomotion. On lightweight mobile robots, however, they face a trilemma of limited scene transferability, restricted onboard computation resources, and sensor hardware cost. To address these issues, we propose a knowledge distillation approach that transfers knowledge from an information-rich, appearance invariant omniview depth policy to a lightweight monocular policy. The key idea is to train the student not only to mimic the expert actions but also to align with the latent embeddings of the omni view depth teacher. Experiments demonstrate that omni-view and depth inputs improve the scene transfer and navigation performance, and that the proposed distillation method enhances the performance of a singleview monocular policy, compared with policies solely imitating actions. Real world experiments further validate the effectiveness and practicality of our approach. Code will be released publicly.

</details>


### [290] [Swim2Real: VLM-Guided System Identification for Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20827)
*Kevin Qiu,Kyle Walker,Mike Y. Michelis,Marek Cygan,Josie Hughes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Swim2Real利用视觉-语言模型从游泳视频中直接反馈，自动校准16参数的软体机器鱼仿真器，无需手动设计搜索步骤，显著缩小了水下机器人仿真到现实的差距，并实现零样本强化学习策略向真实机器人的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于流体-结构耦合的非线性特性、简化流体模型带来的仿真与现实差距以及实验复现困难，软体水下机器人的仿真校准极具挑战，传统方法依赖多阶段手工设计流程，效率低且难以推广。

Method: 提出Swim2Real管道，使用视觉-语言模型（VLM）对比真实与模拟游泳视频，自动生成参数更新建议，并结合回溯线搜索验证步长，提升参数更新接受率，实现16个参数的同步校准。

Result: 校准后仿真器在所有电机频率下均最接近真实鱼的速度（MAE = 7.4 mm/s，比次优方法低43%），五次运行无异常种子；训练策略以50Hz成功迁移到实体机器人；下游强化学习策略游泳距离比BayesOpt方法远12%，比CMA-ES远90%。

Conclusion: VLM引导的校准可直接从视频中有效缩小水下机器人仿真与现实之间的差距，支持零样本策略迁移，无需人工系统辨识，推动了水下机器人通用仿真器自动调参的发展。

Abstract: We present Swim2Real, a pipeline that calibrates a 16-parameter robotic fish simulator from swimming videos using vision-language model (VLM) feedback, requiring no hand-designed search stages. Calibrating soft aquatic robots is particularly challenging because nonlinear fluid-structure coupling makes the parameter landscape chaotic, simplified fluid models introduce a persistent sim-to-real gap, and controlled aquatic experiments are difficult to reproduce. Prior work on this platform required three manually tailored stages to handle this complexity. The VLM compares simulated and real videos and proposes parameter updates. A backtracking line search then validates each step size, tripling the accept rate from 14% to 42% by recovering proposals where the direction is correct but the magnitude is too large. Swim2Real calibrates all 16 parameters simultaneously, most closely matching real fish velocities across all motor frequencies (MAE = 7.4 mm/s, 43% lower than the next-best method), with zero outlier seeds across five runs. Motor commands from the trained policy transfer to the physical fish at 50 Hz, completing the pipeline from swimming video to real-world deployment. Downstream RL policies swim 12% farther than those from BayesOpt-calibrated simulators and 90% farther than CMA-ES. These results demonstrate that VLM-guided calibration can close the sim-to-real gap for aquatic robots directly from video, enabling zero-shot RL transfer to physical swimmers without manual system identification, a step toward automated, general-purpose simulator tuning for underwater robotics.

</details>


### [291] [Characterizing the onset and offset of motor imagery during passive arm movements induced by an upper-body exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20885)
*Kanishka Mitra,Frigyes Samuel Racz,Satyam Kumar,Ashish D. Deshpande,José del R. Millán*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探索了在康复外骨骼存在的情况下，通过非侵入式脑机接口检测运动意图的可行性，重点识别运动想象的起始与终止，结果表明该方法具有可靠的控制潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合机器人与脑机接口技术用于运动康复具有巨大临床潜力，但如何在器械噪声和被动运动干扰下准确检测用户运动意图是一个关键挑战。

Method: 十名参与者在外骨骼诱导的被动臂运动期间进行右臂运动想象，同步LED提示任务起止；利用脑电图信号构建解码器，以识别静息到运动想象开始、维持到结束的转换状态。

Result: 离线解码器评估显示平均起始准确率为60.7%，终止准确率为66.6%；伪在线评估能复现该性能，表明未来可实现可靠的在线外骨骼控制。

Conclusion: 参与者在外骨骼引起的噪声和被动运动下仍能产生高质量且可靠的传感器运动节律，证明了该方法在辅助设备脑控中的可行性与前景。

Abstract: Two distinct technologies have gained attention lately due to their prospects for motor rehabilitation: robotics and brain-machine interfaces (BMIs). Harnessing their combined efforts is a largely uncharted and promising direction that has immense clinical potential. However, a significant challenge is whether motor intentions from the user can be accurately detected using non-invasive BMIs in the presence of instrumental noise and passive movements induced by the rehabilitation exoskeleton. As an alternative to the straightforward continuous control approach, this study instead aims to characterize the onset and offset of motor imagery during passive arm movements induced by an upper-body exoskeleton to allow for the natural control (initiation and termination) of functional movements. Ten participants were recruited to perform kinesthetic motor imagery (MI) of the right arm while attached to the robot, simultaneously cued with LEDs indicating the initiation and termination of a goal-oriented reaching task. Using electroencephalogram signals, we built a decoder to detect the transition between i) rest and beginning MI and ii) maintaining and ending MI. Offline decoder evaluation achieved group average onset accuracy of 60.7% and 66.6% for offset accuracy, revealing that the start and stop of MI could be identified while attached to the robot. Furthermore, pseudo-online evaluation could replicate this performance, forecasting reliable online exoskeleton control in the future. Our approach showed that participants could produce quality and reliable sensorimotor rhythms regardless of noise or passive arm movements induced by wearing the exoskeleton, which opens new possibilities for BMI control of assistive devices.

</details>


### [292] [Implementing Robust M-Estimators with Certifiable Factor Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20932)
*Zhexin Xu,Hanna Jiamei Zhang,Helena Calatrava,Pau Closas,David M. Rosen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合自适应重加权M估计与可验证全局最优的加权最小二乘求解器的方法，用于解决机器人学和计算机视觉中存在外点和非凸优化问题的参数估计任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于异常值和非凸优化景观的存在，传统参数估计方法难以获得全局最优解；M估计虽能提升鲁棒性，但导致优化问题高度非凸，现有方法依赖于对子问题的有效求解，而在旋转、位姿等流形上仍具挑战。

Method: 采用自适应重加权框架，将M估计分解为一系列加权最小二乘子问题，并利用基于因子图的可验证优化技术，在平滑流形上通过快速局部优化即可提供全局最优性证明，且兼容现有因子图软件库。

Result: 在位姿图优化和路标SLAM任务上的实验表明，该方法相比其他基于局部搜索的方法能提供更高质量的估计结果，并可扩展到实际规模的问题。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了鲁棒估计与可验证优化的有效结合，能够在复杂非凸场景下稳定求解参数估计问题，具有良好的实用性与集成性。

Abstract: Parameter estimation in robotics and computer vision faces formidable challenges from both outlier contamination and nonconvex optimization landscapes. While M-estimation addresses the problem of outliers through robust loss functions, it creates severely nonconvex problems that are difficult to solve globally. Adaptive reweighting schemes provide one particularly appealing strategy for implementing M-estimation in practice: these methods solve a sequence of simpler weighted least squares (WLS) subproblems, enabling both the use of standard least squares solvers and the recovery of higher-quality estimates than simple local search. However, adaptive reweighting still crucially relies upon solving the inner WLS problems effectively, a task that remains challenging in many robotics applications due to the intrinsic nonconvexity of many common parameter spaces (e.g. rotations and poses).
  In this paper, we show how one can easily implement adaptively reweighted M-estimators with certifiably correct solvers for the inner WLS subproblems using only fast local optimization over smooth manifolds. Our approach exploits recent work on certifiable factor graph optimization to provide global optimality certificates for the inner WLS subproblems while seamlessly integrating into existing factor graph-based software libraries and workflows. Experimental evaluation on pose-graph optimization and landmark SLAM tasks demonstrates that our adaptively reweighted certifiable estimation approach provides higher-quality estimates than alternative local search-based methods, while scaling tractably to realistic problem sizes.

</details>


### [293] [Cortical Policy: A Dual-Stream View Transformer for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21051)
*Xuening Zhang,Qi Lv,Xiang Deng,Miao Zhang,Xingbo Liu,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受大脑皮层启发的双流视图变换器Cortical Policy，用于机器人操作，通过结合静态视图和动态视图流提升空间推理与动态适应能力，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视图变换器在处理多视角观测时缺乏足够的3D空间推理能力和动态适应性，受人类大脑整合静态与动态视觉信息机制的启发，本文旨在提升机器人在复杂空间和动态环境下的操作性能。

Method: 提出Cortical Policy，包含两个分支：静态视图流利用预训练3D基础模型提取几何一致的关键点特征以增强空间理解；动态视图流通过位置感知的自我中心注视估计模型预训练，模拟大脑背侧通路实现自适应调整，并将两者表征融合以决策动作。

Result: 在RLBench、COLOSSEUM基准和真实世界任务上的实验表明，该方法显著优于当前最先进的基线方法，尤其在语言条件下的复杂动态任务中表现突出。

Conclusion: 双流架构在视觉运动控制中具有优越性，所提出的皮层启发框架为机器人操作提供了新思路，并有望广泛应用于基于视觉的机器人控制。

Abstract: View transformers process multi-view observations to predict actions and have shown impressive performance in robotic manipulation. Existing methods typically extract static visual representations in a view-specific manner, leading to inadequate 3D spatial reasoning ability and a lack of dynamic adaptation. Taking inspiration from how the human brain integrates static and dynamic views to address these challenges, we propose Cortical Policy, a novel dual-stream view transformer for robotic manipulation that jointly reasons from static-view and dynamic-view streams. The static-view stream enhances spatial understanding by aligning features of geometrically consistent keypoints extracted from a pretrained 3D foundation model. The dynamic-view stream achieves adaptive adjustment through position-aware pretraining of an egocentric gaze estimation model, computationally replicating the human cortical dorsal pathway. Subsequently, the complementary view representations of both streams are integrated to determine the final actions, enabling the model to handle spatially-complex and dynamically-changing tasks under language conditions. Empirical evaluations on RLBench, the challenging COLOSSEUM benchmark, and real-world tasks demonstrate that Cortical Policy outperforms state-of-the-art baselines substantially, validating the superiority of dual-stream design for visuomotor control. Our cortex-inspired framework offers a fresh perspective for robotic manipulation and holds potential for broader application in vision-based robot control.

</details>


### [294] [VisFly-Lab: Unified Differentiable Framework for First-Order Reinforcement Learning of Quadrotor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21123)
*Fanxing Li,Fangyu Sun,Tianbao Zhang,Shuyu Wu,Dexin Zuo,yufei Yan,Wenxian Yu,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的可微分框架用于多任务四旋翼控制，并设计了改进的ABPT算法以解决一阶训练中的状态覆盖不足和梯度偏差问题，实验验证了其在仿真和真实场景中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于一阶强化学习与可微分仿真的四旋翼控制研究分散于特定任务中，缺乏系统性的开发与评估框架，限制了实际进展。

Method: 构建了一个封装式、可扩展的统一可微分框架，支持悬停、跟踪、着陆和竞速四个典型任务；提出Amended Backpropagation Through Time (ABPT) 方法，结合可微 rollout 优化、基于值的辅助目标和已访问状态初始化，以缓解标准一阶训练中的状态覆盖有限和梯度偏差问题。

Result: 实验表明ABPT在具有部分不可微奖励的任务中表现显著提升，在完全可微设置下也具竞争力；并通过实机部署验证了策略从仿真到现实的初步可迁移性。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一框架和ABPT算法为多任务四旋翼控制提供了一个系统化、部署导向的解决方案，推动了一阶强化学习在实际场景中的应用潜力。

Abstract: First-order reinforcement learning with differentiable simulation is promising for quadrotor control, but practical progress remains fragmented across task-specific settings. To support more systematic development and evaluation, we present a unified differentiable framework for multi-task quadrotor control. The framework is wrapped, extensible, and equipped with deployment-oriented dynamics, providing a common interface across four representative tasks: hovering, tracking, landing, and racing. We also present the suite of first-order learning algorithms, where we identify two practical bottlenecks of standard first-order training: limited state coverage caused by horizon initialization and gradient bias caused by partially non-differentiable rewards. To address these issues, we propose Amended Backpropagation Through Time (ABPT), which combines differentiable rollout optimization, a value-based auxiliary objective, and visited-state initialization to improve training robustness. Experimental results show that ABPT yields the clearest gains in tasks with partially non-differentiable rewards, while remaining competitive in fully differentiable settings. We further provide proof-of-concept real-world deployments showing initial transferability of policies learned in the proposed framework beyond simulation.

</details>


### [295] [Dynamic Control Barrier Function Regulation with Vision-Language Models for Safe, Adaptive, and Realtime Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21142)
*Jeffrey Chen,Rohan Chandra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AlphaAdj的视觉到控制导航框架，利用单目RGB图像实时调整控制屏障函数（CBF）的安全过滤器保守性，以在动态环境中实现安全与效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的CBF方法使用固定参数，导致在简单场景中过于保守，在危险区域又可能不够安全，难以适应复杂多变的真实环境。

Method: 通过视觉-语言模型（VLM）从当前摄像头画面生成有界的标量风险估计，并将其映射为CBF参数的动态更新；同时采用几何感知的速度相关动态上限和老化门控融合策略，降低推理延迟影响。

Result: 在多种静态和动态障碍物场景中测试表明，AlphaAdj在保持零碰撞的同时，相比固定参数设置效率提升达18.5%，路径更短、到达目标时间更少，并且比无上限基线具有更高的鲁棒性和成功率。
