Conclusion: AlphaAdj能够根据环境风险自适应调节安全约束强度，在保证安全性的同时显著提升机器人导航效率，适用于感知受限的非结构化动态环境。

Abstract: Robots operating in dynamic, unstructured environments must balance safety and efficiency under potentially limited sensing. While control barrier functions (CBFs) provide principled collision avoidance via safety filtering, their behavior is often governed by fixed parameters that can be overly conservative in benign scenes or overly permissive near hazards. We present AlphaAdj, a vision-to-control navigation framework that uses egocentric RGB input to adapt the conservativeness of a CBF safety filter in real time. A vision-language model(VLM) produces a bounded scalar risk estimate from the current camera view, which we map to dynamically update a CBF parameter that modulates how strongly safety constraints are enforced. To address asynchronous inference and non-trivial VLM latency in practice, we combine a geometric, speed-aware dynamic cap and a staleness-gated fusion policy with lightweight implementation choices that reduce end-to-end inference overhead. We evaluate AlphaAdj across multiple static and dynamic obstacle scenarios in a variety of environments, comparing against fixed-parameter and uncapped ablations. Results show that AlphaAdj maintains collision-free navigation while improving efficiency (in terms of path length and time to goal) by up to 18.5% relative to fixed settings and improving robustness and success rate relative to an uncapped baseline.

</details>


### [296] [Affordance-Guided Enveloping Grasp Demonstration Toward Non-destructive Disassembly of Pinch-Infeasible Mating Parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21143)
*Masaki Tsutsumi,Takuya Kiyokawa,Gen Sako,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于可操作性模板（Affordance Templates）的遥操作方法，通过物理仿真预生成包络抓取候选，并以可视化方式辅助操作员在复杂约束下选择有效的抓取策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于视觉遮挡和几何约束，仅依赖2D相机难以教授适用于复杂装配件拆卸的多指包络抓取动作。

Method: 通过物理仿真预先生成包络抓取候选，形成反映抓取质量的彩色梯度可视化可操作性模板（ATs），并将其用于增强操作员感知的遥操作框架。

Result: 仿真验证了该方法在多种组件上的通用性；真实机器人实验表明，AT增强的可视化能帮助操作员在严重视觉和几何约束下有效选择和教授包络抓取策略。

Conclusion: 所提出的可操作性模板可视化方法显著提升了遥操作中包络抓取的教学效率与成功率，适用于复杂环境下的机器人拆卸任务。

Abstract: Robotic disassembly of complex mating components often renders pinch grasping infeasible, necessitating multi-fingered enveloping grasps. However, visual occlusions and geometric constraints complicate teaching appropriate grasp motions when relying solely on 2D camera feeds. To address this, we propose an affordance-guided teleoperation method that pre-generates enveloping grasp candidates via physics simulation. These Affordance Templates (ATs) are visualized with a color gradient reflecting grasp quality to augment operator perception. Simulations demonstrate the method's generality across various components. Real-robot experiments validate that AT-based visual augmentation enables operators to effectively select and teach enveloping grasp strategies for real-world disassembly, even under severe visual and geometric constraints.

</details>


### [297] [Architecture for Multi-Unmanned Aerial Vehicles based Autonomous Precision Agriculture Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21183)
*Ebasa Temesgen,Nathnael Minyelshowa,Lebsework Negash*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于精准农业中多无人机（UAV）协同作业的自主化端到端架构，涵盖任务建模、路径规划、图像处理和数据采集等功能，具备高效率、容错性和易用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无人机在精准农业中的广泛应用，亟需一个结构化的框架来整合各类算法并实现最小人工干预下的多无人机协同作业。

Method: 通过建模无人机在精准农业中的各项任务（如图像处理、路径规划、通信、数据采集和田地映射），构建了一个以多机协作和低物理干预为前提的系统架构，并考虑了多无人机应用中的各种限制因素。

Result: 仿真和实地测试表明，该架构具有容错性强、鲁棒性高、开发与使用便捷等优点，能够高效支持从任务规划到数据处理的全流程自动化。

Conclusion: 所提出的架构为精准农业提供了高效的多无人机协同解决方案，有助于农民全面部署无人机技术于实际耕作中。

Abstract: The use of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) in precision agriculture has seen a huge increase recently. As such, systems that aim to apply various algorithms on the field need a structured framework of abstractions. This paper defines the various tasks of the UAVs in precision agriculture and model them into an architectural framework. The presented architecture is built on the context that there will be minimal physical intervention to do the tasks defined with multiple coordinated and cooperative UAVs. Various tasks such as image processing, path planning, communication, data acquisition, and field mapping are employed in the architecture to provide an efficient system. Besides, different limitation for applying Multi-UAVs in precision agriculture has been considered in designing the architecture. The architecture provides an autonomous end-to-end solution, starting from mission planning, data acquisition and image processing framework that is highly efficient and can enable farmers to comprehensively deploy UAVs onto their lands. Simulation and field tests shows that the architecture offers a number of advantages that include fault-tolerance, robustness, developer and user-friendliness.

</details>


### [298] [GAPG: Geometry Aware Push-Grasping Synergy for Goal-Oriented Manipulation in Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21195)
*Lijingze Xiao,Jinhong Du,Yang Cong,Supeng Diao,Yu Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种几何感知的推抓协同框架，利用点云数据整合抓取和推动评估，提高了机器人在杂乱环境中操作的安全性、效率和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱环境中，传统单步抓取常不可行，现有推动辅助方法因忽略几何信息而影响稳定性和效率。

Method: 利用点云数据设计抓取评估模块分析夹爪与物体间的几何关系，并通过推动评估模块预测推动对可抓取空间的影响，实现推抓协同决策。

Result: 在仿真和真实场景中广泛测试，结果表明该方法能有效将不可抓取状态转化为可抓取状态，且对未见物体具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架通过联合考虑抓取和推动中的几何信息，显著提升了复杂场景下机器人操作的鲁棒性和实用性。

Abstract: Grasping target objects is a fundamental skill for robotic manipulation, but in cluttered environments with stacked or occluded objects, a single-step grasp is often insufficient. To address this, previous work has introduced pushing as an auxiliary action to create graspable space. However, these methods often struggle with both stability and efficiency because they neglect the scene's geometric information, which is essential for evaluating grasp robustness and ensuring that pushing actions are safe and effective. To this end, we propose a geometry-aware push-grasp synergy framework that leverages point cloud data to integrate grasp and push evaluation. Specifically, the grasp evaluation module analyzes the geometric relationship between the gripper's point cloud and the points enclosed within its closing region to determine grasp feasibility and stability. Guided by this, the push evaluation module predicts how pushing actions influence future graspable space, enabling the robot to select actions that reliably transform non-graspable states into graspable ones. By jointly reasoning about geometry in both grasping and pushing, our framework achieves safer, more efficient, and more reliable manipulation in cluttered settings. Our method is extensively tested in simulation and real-world environments in various scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that our model generalizes well to real-world scenes and unseen objects.

</details>


### [299] [Evaluating Factor-Wise Auxiliary Dynamics Supervision for Latent Structure and Robustness in Simulated Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21268)
*Chayanin Chamachot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究评估了在模拟人形机器人行走任务中，因子化辅助动力学监督是否能产生有用的潜在结构或提高鲁棒性。结果表明，DynaMITE模型中的辅助监督并未带来可解释的因子结构或性能提升，实际收益主要来自tanh瓶颈而非辅助损失，LSTM在基准任务中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索因子化辅助监督是否能在强化学习控制任务中诱导出可解释且功能分离的潜在表示，并提升模型在分布外扰动下的鲁棒性。

Method: 在PPO框架下训练DynaMITE（带因子化24维潜在空间的Transformer编码器），并引入每因子辅助损失；与LSTM、普通Transformer和MLP进行对比，在四个Isaac Lab任务上评估其潜在结构可解释性及ID/OOD性能；通过2x2因子消融实验分析tanh瓶颈与辅助损失的独立作用。

Result: DynaMITE的潜在空间无法解码出动力学因子（探针R²≈0），缺乏功能分离性（夹断子空间对奖励影响<0.05），标准解耦指标接近零；辅助损失对ID和OOD奖励均无显著影响（p>0.6），而tanh瓶颈在两类条件下均有小幅提升；LSTM在所有任务中取得最高基准奖励（p<0.03）；DynaMITE在复合扰动下退化较小，但优势源于瓶颈压缩而非辅助监督。

Conclusion: 因子化辅助动力学监督未能产生可解释的潜在结构，也未显著提升性能或鲁棒性；观察到的优势主要来自tanh瓶颈的表示学习效应，而非辅助监督本身；在人形行走任务中，LSTM仍是更优选择。

Abstract: We evaluate whether factor-wise auxiliary dynamics supervision produces useful latent structure or improved robustness in simulated humanoid locomotion. DynaMITE -- a transformer encoder with a factored 24-d latent trained by per-factor auxiliary losses during proximal policy optimization (PPO) -- is compared against Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), plain Transformer, and Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) baselines on a Unitree G1 humanoid across four Isaac Lab tasks. The supervised latent shows no evidence of decodable or functionally separable factor structure: probe R^2 ~ 0 for all five dynamics factors, clamping any subspace changes reward by < 0.05, and standard disentanglement metrics (MIG, DCI, SAP) are near zero. An unsupervised LSTM hidden state achieves higher probe R^2 (up to 0.10). A 2x2 factorial ablation (n = 10 seeds) isolates the contributions of the tanh bottleneck and auxiliary losses: the auxiliary losses show no measurable effect on either in-distribution (ID) reward (+0.03, p = 0.732) or severe out-of-distribution (OOD) reward (+0.03, p = 0.669), while the bottleneck shows a small, consistent advantage in both regimes (ID: +0.16, p = 0.207; OOD: +0.10, p = 0.208). The bottleneck advantage persists under severe combined perturbation but does not amplify, indicating a training-time representation benefit rather than a robustness mechanism. LSTM achieves the best nominal reward on all four tasks (p < 0.03); DynaMITE degrades less under combined-shift stress (2.3% vs. 16.7%), but this difference is attributable to the bottleneck compression, not the auxiliary supervision. For locomotion practitioners: auxiliary dynamics supervision does not produce an interpretable estimator and does not measurably improve reward or robustness beyond what the bottleneck alone provides; recurrent baselines remain the stronger choice for nominal performance.

</details>


### [300] [DyGeoVLN: Infusing Dynamic Geometry Foundation Model into Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21269)
*Xiangchen Liu,Hanghan Zheng,Jeil Jeong,Minsung Yoon,Lin Zhao,Zhide Zhong,Haoang Li,Sung-Eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态几何感知的视觉-语言导航框架DyGeoVLN，通过引入动态几何基础模型和自适应分辨率的令牌剪枝策略，在动态环境中实现了先进的性能和强健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言导航方法多基于静态场景假设，难以在动态、真实世界场景中泛化，因此需要一种能处理动态环境的新方法。

Method: 将动态几何基础模型通过跨分支特征融合集成到VLN框架中，并引入一种无需姿态信息且自适应分辨率的令牌剪枝策略以压缩历史令牌信息。

Result: 在多个基准测试上取得了最先进的性能，并在真实世界环境中表现出较强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DyGeoVLN通过显式的3D空间表示和视觉-语义推理，有效提升了动态环境下的视觉-语言导航能力。

Abstract: Vision-language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to understand visual observations and language instructions to navigate in unseen environments. Most existing approaches rely on static scene assumptions and struggle to generalize in dynamic, real-world scenarios. To address this challenge, we propose DyGeoVLN, a dynamic geometry-aware VLN framework. Our method infuses a dynamic geometry foundation model into the VLN framework through cross-branch feature fusion to enable explicit 3D spatial representation and visual-semantic reasoning. To efficiently compress historical token information in long-horizon, dynamic navigation, we further introduce a novel pose-free and adaptive-resolution token-pruning strategy. This strategy can remove spatio-temporal redundant tokens to reduce inference cost. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple benchmarks and exhibits strong robustness in real-world environments.

</details>


### [301] [Bayesian Active Object Recognition and 6D Pose Estimation from Multimodal Contact Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21410)
*Haodong Zheng,Gabriele M. Caddeo,Andrei C. Jalba,Wijnand A. IJsselsteijn,Lorenzo Natale,Raymond H. Cuijpers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合腕部力/力矩感知、GelSight触觉感知和自由空间约束的主动触觉探索框架，用于联合物体识别与6D姿态估计，在模拟和真实机器人实验中验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决纯基于力/力矩信息进行物体识别和6D姿态估计时存在的歧义性和鲁棒性不足问题。

Method: 在贝叶斯推理框架下融合接触与非接触证据，采用定制化粒子滤波器进行高效推断，并利用推断结果在可达性约束下选择信息量最大的下一步触摸动作，同时开发了运动规划与控制框架以实现触觉伺服和路径执行。

Result: 在仿真和Franka Panda机器人上对11个YCB物体的实验表明，相比仅使用力/力矩的基线方法，所提方法显著提高了识别和姿态估计的准确性与稳定性，并减少了动作循环次数。

Conclusion: 该框架通过多模态触觉感知与主动探索策略的有效集成，实现了更准确、稳定且高效的联合物体识别与6D姿态估计。

Abstract: We present an active tactile exploration framework for joint object recognition and 6D pose estimation. The proposed method integrates wrist force/torque sensing, GelSight tactile sensing, and free-space constraints within a Bayesian inference framework that maintains a belief over object class and pose during active tactile exploration. By combining contact and non-contact evidence, the framework reduces ambiguity and improves robustness in the joint class-pose estimation problem. To enable efficient inference in the large hypothesis space, we employ a customized particle filter that progressively samples particles based on new observations. The inferred belief is further used to guide active exploration by selecting informative next touches under reachability constraints. For effective data collection, a motion planning and control framework is developed to plan and execute feasible paths for tactile exploration, handle unexpected contacts and GelSight-surface alignment with tactile servoing. We evaluate the framework in simulation and on a Franka Panda robot using 11 YCB objects. Results show that incorporating tactile and free-space information substantially improves recognition and pose estimation accuracy and stability, while reducing the number of action cycles compared with force/torque-only baselines. Code, dataset, and supplementary material will be made available online.

</details>


### [302] [HyReach: Vision-Guided Hybrid Manipulator Reaching in Unseen Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21421)
*Shivani Kamtikar,Kendall Koe,Justin Wasserman,Samhita Marri,Benjamin Walt,Naveen Kumar Uppalapati,Girish Krishnan,Girish Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种实时刚-软连续体混合机械臂系统，结合视觉感知与形状感知运动规划，实现在未知复杂环境中的鲁棒目标到达，无需环境重训练且定位误差小于2 cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了应对机器人在非结构化、杂乱且未知环境中操作的挑战，需要兼具柔顺性、适应性和精确控制能力的机械臂系统。

Method: 融合视觉感知与3D场景重建，结合形状感知的运动规划生成安全轨迹，并采用基于学习的控制器驱动刚-软混合臂到达任意目标位姿。

Result: 在多种真实杂乱场景中实现了稳定的目标到达性能，末端定位误差始终低于2 cm，且无需针对特定环境重新训练。

Conclusion: 该混合刚-软连续体操纵器系统具备良好的泛化能力和鲁棒性，适用于非结构化环境下的自适应可靠操作。

Abstract: As robotic systems increasingly operate in unstructured, cluttered, and previously unseen environments, there is a growing need for manipulators that combine compliance, adaptability, and precise control. This work presents a real-time hybrid rigid-soft continuum manipulator system designed for robust open-world object reaching in such challenging environments. The system integrates vision-based perception and 3D scene reconstruction with shape-aware motion planning to generate safe trajectories. A learning-based controller drives the hybrid arm to arbitrary target poses, leveraging the flexibility of the soft segment while maintaining the precision of the rigid segment. The system operates without environment-specific retraining, enabling direct generalization to new scenes. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate consistent reaching performance with errors below 2 cm across diverse cluttered setups, highlighting the potential of hybrid manipulators for adaptive and reliable operation in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [303] [GaussianSSC: Triplane-Guided Directional Gaussian Fields for 3D Semantic Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21487)
*Ruiqi Xian,Jing Liang,He Yin,Xuewei Qi,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaussianSSC是一种两阶段、基于网格且受三平面引导的语义场景补全方法，通过引入高斯锚定和高斯-三平面优化模块，在不替换体素网格的情况下提升单目占据估计和语义预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在保持体素网格效率的同时，利用高斯特性改善体素与图像的对齐以及占据估计精度。

Method: 提出Gaussian Anchoring实现亚像素级图像聚合，并将体素特征转化为每体素高斯场，通过三平面对其的高斯-三平面优化模块进行局部聚集和全局聚合。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI数据集上，第一阶段占据估计的召回率提升+1.0%，精确率+2.0%，IoU+1.8%；第二阶段语义预测IoU提升+1.8%，mIoU提升+0.8%。

Conclusion: GaussianSSC有效结合了高斯特性与三平面表示，在不增加复杂结构的前提下显著提升了语义场景补全的性能。

Abstract: We present \emph{GaussianSSC}, a two-stage, grid-native and triplane-guided approach to semantic scene completion (SSC) that injects the benefits of Gaussians without replacing the voxel grid or maintaining a separate Gaussian set. We introduce \emph{Gaussian Anchoring}, a sub-pixel, Gaussian-weighted image aggregation over fused FPN features that tightens voxel--image alignment and improves monocular occupancy estimation. We further convert point-like voxel features into a learned per-voxel Gaussian field and refine triplane features via a triplane-aligned \emph{Gaussian--Triplane Refinement} module that combines \emph{local gathering} (target-centric) and \emph{global aggregation} (source-centric). This directional, anisotropic support captures surface tangency, scale, and occlusion-aware asymmetry while preserving the efficiency of triplane representations. On SemanticKITTI~\cite{behley2019semantickitti}, GaussianSSC improves Stage~1 occupancy by +1.0\% Recall, +2.0\% Precision, and +1.8\% IoU over state-of-the-art baselines, and improves Stage~2 semantic prediction by +1.8\% IoU and +0.8\% mIoU.

</details>


### [304] [A Framework for Closed-Loop Robotic Assembly, Alignment and Self-Recovery of Precision Optical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21496)
*Seou Choi,Sachin Vaidya,Caio Silva,Shiekh Zia Uddin,Sajib Biswas Shuvo,Shrish Choudhary,Marin Soljačić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种用于高精度光学系统自主构建、对准和维护的机器人框架，实现了从随机分布元件中全自动搭建桌面激光腔的演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由空间光学系统由于其严格的对准要求和复杂的物理参数耦合，长期依赖人工操作，自动化难度大，限制了实验效率与可扩展性。

Method: 结合分层计算机视觉系统、优化算法和定制化工具，实现光学元件的识别、定位、装配与闭环调优。

Result: 成功实现了桌面激光腔的全自主构建，包括光束中心对准、多光束空间对齐、谐振腔调校、激光模式选择及在扰动后的自恢复。

Conclusion: 该框架为高精度光学系统的自动化提供了通用解决方案，为跨领域的自主光学实验奠定了基础。

Abstract: Robotic automation has transformed scientific workflows in domains such as chemistry and materials science, yet free-space optics, which is a high precision domain, remains largely manual. Optical systems impose strict spatial and angular tolerances, and their performance is governed by tightly coupled physical parameters, making generalizable automation particularly challenging. In this work, we present a robotics framework for the autonomous construction, alignment, and maintenance of precision optical systems. Our approach integrates hierarchical computer vision systems, optimization routines, and custom-built tools to achieve this functionality. As a representative demonstration, we perform the fully autonomous construction of a tabletop laser cavity from randomly distributed components. The system performs several tasks such as laser beam centering, spatial alignment of multiple beams, resonator alignment, laser mode selection, and self-recovery from induced misalignment and disturbances. By achieving closed-loop autonomy for highly sensitive optical systems, this work establishes a foundation for autonomous optical experiments for applications across technical domains.

</details>


### [305] [Auction-Based Task Allocation with Energy-Conscientious Trajectory Optimization for AMR Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21545)
*Jiachen Li,Soovadeep Bakshi,Jian Chu,Shihao Li,Dongmei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于非对称任务空间中多机器人任务分配与轨迹优化的分层两阶段框架，通过顺序拍卖和最优控制实现节能，并根据环境特性选择距离或能量感知出价策略以优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非对称任务空间中，传统任务分配方法未能充分考虑物理能耗模型与动态环境变化（如摩擦异质性），导致能效次优。本文旨在设计一个兼顾能耗精度与实时性的多机器人协同框架。

Method: 采用分层两阶段方法：第一阶段使用闭式出价函数的顺序拍卖进行任务分配；第二阶段各机器人基于物理电池模型独立求解最小能耗轨迹的最优控制问题，并通过成对避障修正轨迹。引入事件触发的暖启动重调度机制应对故障、优先到达和能量偏差。

Result: 在505个场景中测试了2-20台机器人和最多100个任务，两种拍卖变体平均比最近任务分配节能11.8%，重调度延迟低于10ms。在均匀环境中，距离出价比能量出价优3.5%（p < 0.05），因闭式近似误差15.7%导致出价排序准确率降至87%；而在摩擦异质性强（能量-距离相关性r < 0.85）时，区域感知的能量出价优于距离出价2-2.4%。

Conclusion: 出价度量的性能具有情境依赖性：在地形近似均匀时应使用距离出价，而在摩擦变化显著时应采用能量感知出价，为实际部署提供了明确指导。

Abstract: This paper presents a hierarchical two-stage framework for multi-robot task allocation and trajectory optimization in asymmetric task spaces: (1) a sequential auction allocates tasks using closed-form bid functions, and (2) each robot independently solves an optimal control problem for energy-minimal trajectories with a physics-based battery model, followed by a collision avoidance refinement step using pairwise proximity penalties. Event-triggered warm-start rescheduling with bounded trigger frequency handles robot faults, priority arrivals, and energy deviations. Across 505 scenarios with 2-20 robots and up to 100 tasks on three factory layouts, both energy- and distance-based auction variants achieve 11.8% average energy savings over nearest-task allocation, with rescheduling latency under 10 ms. The central finding is that bid-metric performance is regime-dependent: in uniform workspaces, distance bids outperform energy bids by 3.5% (p < 0.05, Wilcoxon) because a 15.7% closed-form approximation error degrades bid ranking accuracy to 87%; however, when workspace friction heterogeneity is sufficient (r < 0.85 energy-distance correlation), a zone-aware energy bid outperforms distance bids by 2-2.4%. These results provide practitioner guidance: use distance bids in near-uniform terrain and energy-aware bids when friction variation is significant.

</details>


### [306] [Conformal Koopman for Embedded Nonlinear Control with Statistical Robustness: Theory and Real-World Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21580)
*Koki Hirano,Hiroyasu Tsukamoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Koopman算子的全数据驱动框架，结合收缩理论与共形预测，为非线性系统提供闭环控制下的状态跟踪误差概率界，确保安全性与鲁棒性，并在仿真和真实扑翼无人机实验中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Koopman-based控制方法在建模不确定性下缺乏对跟踪误差的形式化保证，且多为开环结构，难以兼顾安全性和闭环性能。

Method: 通过建立Koopman算子与收缩理论的联系，利用共形预测推导出与状态相关的建模不确定性边界，并构建考虑前向与逆向建模误差的闭环控制架构，从而获得分布无关的概率性跟踪误差界。

Result: 在Dubins车仿真和高度非线性的扑翼无人机实测中，所提方法实现了精确跟踪并提供了形式化的安全保证，验证了其在建模不确定性下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架为Koopman-based控制提供了可量化的性能提升手段，在无需假设误差分布的前提下实现了闭环安全性与跟踪精度的统一。

Abstract: We propose a fully data-driven, Koopman-based framework for statistically robust control of discrete-time nonlinear systems with linear embeddings. Establishing a connection between the Koopman operator and contraction theory, it offers distribution-free probabilistic bounds on the state tracking error under Koopman modeling uncertainty. Conformal prediction is employed here to rigorously derive a bound on the state-dependent modeling uncertainty throughout the trajectory, ensuring safety and robustness without assuming a specific error prediction structure or distribution. Unlike prior approaches that merely combine conformal prediction with Koopman-based control in an open-loop setting, our method establishes a closed-loop control architecture with formal guarantees that explicitly account for both forward and inverse modeling errors. Also, by expressing the tracking error bound in terms of the control parameters and the modeling errors, our framework offers a quantitative means to formally enhance the performance of arbitrary Koopman-based control. We validate our method both in numerical simulations with the Dubins car and in real-world experiments with a highly nonlinear flapping-wing drone. The results demonstrate that our method indeed provides formal safety guarantees while maintaining accurate tracking performance under Koopman modeling uncertainty.

</details>


### [307] [RTD-RAX: Fast, Safe Trajectory Planning for Systems under Unknown Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21635)
*Evanns Morales-Cuadrado,Long Kiu Chung,Shreyas Kousik,Samuel Coogan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了RTD-RAX，一种基于运行时保障的轨迹规划框架，通过非保守的候选轨迹生成和混合单调可达性分析实现快速、抗扰的安全认证，并在轨迹不安全时进行在线修复，确保系统在实时干扰下仍能安全地向目标推进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的基于可达性的轨迹设计（RTD）方法存在保守性强和无法应对实时扰动的问题，限制了其在复杂动态环境中的应用。

Method: 提出RTD-RAX框架：使用非保守RTD生成目标导向的候选轨迹，采用混合单调可达性进行快速、考虑扰动的在线安全验证，并在轨迹失败时执行保持进度的安全修复机制。

Result: 实现了更少保守性、更强鲁棒性和实时适应扰动的能力，能够在存在实时不确定性的情况下快速生成并验证安全轨迹。

Conclusion: RTD-RAX有效克服了传统RTD的局限性，在保证安全性的同时提升了性能和实用性，适用于需要高实时性和安全性的自主系统。

Abstract: Reachability-based Trajectory Design (RTD) is a provably safe, real-time trajectory planning framework that combines offline reachable-set computation with online trajectory optimization. However, standard RTD implementations suffer from two key limitations: conservatism induced by worst-case reachable-set overapproximations, and an inability to account for real-time disturbances during execution. This paper presents RTD-RAX, a runtime-assurance extension of RTD that utilizes a non-conservative RTD formulation to rapidly generate goal-directed candidate trajectories, and utilizes mixed monotone reachability for fast, disturbance-aware online safety certification. When proposed trajectories fail safety certification under real-time uncertainty, a repair procedure finds nearby safe trajectories that preserve progress toward the goal while guaranteeing safety under real-time disturbances.

</details>


### [308] [PRM-as-a-Judge: A Dense Evaluation Paradigm for Fine-Grained Robotic Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21669)
*Yuheng Ji,Yuyang Liu,Huajie Tan,Xuchuan Huang,Fanding Huang,Yijie Xu,Cheng Chi,Yuting Zhao,Huaihai Lyu,Peterson Co,Mingyu Cao,Qiongyu Zhang,Zhe Li,Enshen Zhou,Pengwei Wang,Zhongyuan Wang,Shanghang Zhang,Xiaolong Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于过程奖励模型（PRM）的密集型机器人执行评估范式 PRM-as-a-Judge，通过轨迹视频评估任务进展，并引入OPD指标体系量化执行质量，揭示了传统仅看结果的评估方法无法发现的行为特征与失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人评估主要依赖二元成功率为指标，忽略了执行过程中的关键信息（如进展、效率和稳定性），难以全面反映策略质量，因此需要一种更细粒度、更丰富的评估方式。

Method: 提出PRM-as-a-Judge范式，利用过程奖励模型从轨迹视频中估计任务进展；构建OPD（结果-过程-诊断）度量系统，基于任务对齐的进展势能来形式化执行质量；提出宏观一致性与微观分辨率两个公理性质用于刻画密集评估的有效性，并在RoboPulse基准上验证微分辨率性能。

Result: 实验表明，基于势能的PRM评估器满足宏观一致性，且在RoboPulse基准上优于基于判别相似性的方法和通用基础模型评估器；使用PRM-as-a-Judge对主流策略范式进行审计，揭示了长视野任务中传统指标无法察觉的行为特征和失败模式。

Conclusion: PRM-as-a-Judge提供了一种自然且有效的密集评估框架，能够从视频轨迹中量化机器人执行过程的质量，OPD指标系统结合公理化性质为未来机器人评估提供了理论基础与实用工具。

Abstract: Current robotic evaluation is still largely dominated by binary success rates, which collapse rich execution processes into a single outcome and obscure critical qualities such as progress, efficiency, and stability. To address this limitation, we propose PRM-as-a-Judge, a dense evaluation paradigm that leverages Process Reward Models (PRMs) to audit policy execution directly from trajectory videos by estimating task progress from observation sequences. Central to this paradigm is the OPD (Outcome-Process-Diagnosis) metric system, which explicitly formalizes execution quality via a task-aligned progress potential. We characterize dense robotic evaluation through two axiomatic properties: macro-consistency, which requires additive and path-consistent aggregation, and micro-resolution, which requires sensitivity to fine-grained physical evolution. Under this formulation, potential-based PRM judges provide a natural instantiation of dense evaluation, with macro-consistency following directly from the induced scalar potential. We empirically validate the micro-resolution property using RoboPulse, a diagnostic benchmark specifically designed for probing micro-scale progress discrimination, where several trajectory-trained PRM judges outperform discriminative similarity-based methods and general-purpose foundation-model judges. Finally, leveraging PRM-as-a-Judge and the OPD metric system, we conduct a structured audit of mainstream policy paradigms across long-horizon tasks, revealing behavioral signatures and failure modes that are invisible to outcome-only metrics.

</details>


### [309] [BiPreManip: Learning Affordance-Based Bimanual Preparatory Manipulation through Anticipatory Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21679)
*Yan Shen,Feng Jiang,Zichen He,Xiaoqi Li,Yuchen Liu,Zhiyu Li,Ruihai Wu,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了协作预备操作（Collaborative Preparatory Manipulation）这一双臂操作任务的新类别，旨在解决日常物体难以直接抓取或功能操作的问题。通过基于视觉功能的框架，先预想最终目标动作，再引导一只手臂执行一系列预备操作以促进另一只手臂的后续操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多日常物体由于其形状或摆放方式难以被机器人直接抓取或功能操作，需要双臂之间的顺序性和非对称性协调。现有方法缺乏对物体语义与几何结构的理解以及长视野动作规划能力，因此需要一种能够实现前瞻性双臂协同的新方法。

Method: 提出一种基于视觉功能（visual affordance）的框架，首先预测最终的目标导向动作，然后据此指导一个机械臂执行一系列预备性操作，为另一个机械臂完成任务创造条件。该方法利用功能中心表示实现双臂间的前瞻性推理与协调，并在模拟和真实环境中进行验证。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界实验中，所提方法相比竞争基线显著提高了任务成功率和跨对象类别的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于视觉功能的协作预备操作框架有效解决了双臂机器人在复杂日常任务中的协调问题，具备良好的泛化性和实用性，推动了具身智能体对物体交互的理解与应用。

Abstract: Many everyday objects are difficult to directly grasp (e.g., a flat iPad) or manipulate functionally (e.g., opening the cap of a pen lying on a desk). Such tasks require sequential, asymmetric coordination between two arms, where one arm performs preparatory manipulation that enables the other's goal-directed action - for instance, pushing the iPad to the table's edge before picking it up, or lifting the pen body to allow the other hand to remove its cap. In this work, we introduce Collaborative Preparatory Manipulation, a class of bimanual manipulation tasks that demand understanding object semantics and geometry, anticipating spatial relationships, and planning long-horizon coordinated actions between the two arms. To tackle this challenge, we propose a visual affordance-based framework that first envisions the final goal-directed action and then guides one arm to perform a sequence of preparatory manipulations that facilitate the other arm's subsequent operation. This affordance-centric representation enables anticipatory inter-arm reasoning and coordination, generalizing effectively across various objects spanning diverse categories. Extensive experiments in both simulation and the real world demonstrate that our approach substantially improves task success rates and generalization compared to competitive baselines.

</details>


### [310] [Can a Robot Walk the Robotic Dog: Triple-Zero Collaborative Navigation for Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21723)
*Yaxuan Wang,Yifan Xiang,Ke Li,Xun Zhang,BoWen Ye,Zhuochen Fan,Fei Wei,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练、无需先验知识、无需仿真的异构多机器人协同框架TZPP，采用协调者-探索者架构，在多种环境中实现了鲁棒且高效的路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现异构多机器人系统在未知环境中无需训练和仿真即可协作的实用化部署。

Method: 采用协调者-探索者架构，由人形机器人负责任务协调，四足机器人负责探索路径，并结合多模态大语言模型提供导航指导。

Result: 在Unitree G1和Go2机器人上实现了TZPP，在室内外多种复杂环境中表现出与人类相当的效率和强适应性。

Conclusion: TZPP通过消除对训练和仿真的依赖，为异构机器人系统的实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present Triple Zero Path Planning (TZPP), a collaborative framework for heterogeneous multi-robot systems that requires zero training, zero prior knowledge, and zero simulation. TZPP employs a coordinator--explorer architecture: a humanoid robot handles task coordination, while a quadruped robot explores and identifies feasible paths using guidance from a multimodal large language model. We implement TZPP on Unitree G1 and Go2 robots and evaluate it across diverse indoor and outdoor environments, including obstacle-rich and landmark-sparse settings. Experiments show that TZPP achieves robust, human-comparable efficiency and strong adaptability to unseen scenarios. By eliminating reliance on training and simulation, TZPP offers a practical path toward real-world deployment of heterogeneous robot cooperation. Our code and video are provided at: https://github.com/triple-zeropp/Triple-zero-robot-agent

</details>


### [311] [Memory-Efficient Boundary Map for Large-Scale Occupancy Grid Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21774)
*Benxu Tang,Yunfan Ren,Yixi Cai,Fanze Kong,Wenyi Liu,Fangcheng Zhu,Longji Yin,Liuyu Shi,Fu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为边界地图（boundary map）的新方法，通过仅维护环境中的边界体素来显著减少高分辨率和大规模场景下的内存消耗，并基于二维表示实现对三维环境中任意位置占据状态的高效查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统占用网格地图方法在高分辨率和大尺度场景下需要大量内存资源，难以满足安全关键型机器人应用的需求。因此，需要一种更节省内存且高效的占据状态表示与查询方法。

Method: 提出一种新的边界地图表示方法，仅显式存储占据体素和前沿体素等边界体素，自由和未知区域由边界内的体积或外部自动表示；设计支持高效查询的数据结构，并提出全局-局部建图框架及相应的更新算法以实现实时传感器数据下的快速构建与更新。

Result: 该方法将三维空间中完整体积的存储转化为二维闭合曲面的表示，显著降低了内存消耗；理论分析证明了其查询算法的有效性，并实现了高效的实时建图与更新。

Conclusion: 边界地图是一种高效、低内存的环境占据表示方法，适用于大规模、高分辨率的机器人应用，且已开源实现以促进社区发展。

Abstract: Determining the occupancy status of locations in the environment is a fundamental task for safety-critical robotic applications. Traditional occupancy grid mapping methods subdivide the environment into a grid of voxels, each associated with one of three occupancy states: free, occupied, or unknown. These methods explicitly maintain all voxels within the mapped volume and determine the occupancy state of a location by directly querying the corresponding voxel that the location falls within. However, maintaining all grid voxels in high-resolution and large-scale scenarios requires substantial memory resources. In this paper, we introduce a novel representation that only maintains the boundary of the mapped volume. Specifically, we explicitly represent the boundary voxels, such as the occupied voxels and frontier voxels, while free and unknown voxels are automatically represented by volumes within or outside the boundary, respectively. As our representation maintains only a closed surface in two-dimensional (2D) space, instead of the entire volume in three-dimensional (3D) space, it significantly reduces memory consumption. Then, based on this 2D representation, we propose a method to determine the occupancy state of arbitrary locations in the 3D environment. We term this method as boundary map. Besides, we design a novel data structure for maintaining the boundary map, supporting efficient occupancy state queries. Theoretical analyses of the occupancy state query algorithm are also provided. Furthermore, to enable efficient construction and updates of the boundary map from the real-time sensor measurements, we propose a global-local mapping framework and corresponding update algorithms. Finally, we will make our implementation of the boundary map open-source on GitHub to benefit the community:https://github.com/hku-mars/BDM.

</details>


### [312] [Directional Mollification for Controlled Smooth Path Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21831)
*Alfredo González-Calvin,Juan F. Jiménez,Héctor García de Marina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的路径生成方法——方向磨光（directional mollification），解决了传统磨光方法无法精确通过预定航路点的问题，同时保持了良好的解析性质，可生成无限可微、严格插值航路点且满足显式曲率约束的平滑路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的磨光方法虽高效且具有良好的数学性质，但其生成的路径被限制在原始路径的凸包内，无法实现对航路点的精确插值，这在需要严格遵循规划点的任务中是一个根本性缺陷。因此，需要一种既能保留磨光优点又能实现精确插值的新方法。

Method: 提出方向磨光算子，通过引入方向性信息扩展经典磨光框架，并构建包含经典与方向磨光在内的参数化路径生成算子族，提供统一的理论框架。该方法利用闭式表达式给出曲率边界，确保路径的光滑性和可行性。

Result: 方向磨光能够生成无限可微的路径，精确通过指定航路点，且可以任意精度逼近原始非光滑输入路径，同时满足显式的曲率上限约束。参数化家族统一了现有方法，增强了路径生成的灵活性和理论完整性。

Conclusion: 方向磨光克服了传统磨光在航路点插值上的局限性，兼具理论严谨性与工程实用性，为自主系统路径生成提供了一个高效、灵活且可分析的新工具。

Abstract: Path generation, the problem of producing smooth, executable paths from discrete planning outputs, such as waypoint sequences, is a fundamental step in the control of autonomous robots, industrial robots, and CNC machines, as path following and trajectory tracking controllers impose strict differentiability requirements on their reference inputs to guarantee stability and convergence, particularly for nonholonomic systems. Mollification has been recently proposed as a computationally efficient and analytically tractable tool for path generation, offering formal smoothness and curvature guarantees with advantages over spline interpolation and optimization-based methods. However, this mollification is subject to a fundamental geometric constraint: the smoothed path is confined within the convex hull of the original path, precluding exact waypoint interpolation, even when explicitly required by mission specifications or upstream planners. We introduce directional mollification, a novel operator that resolves this limitation while retaining the analytical tractability of classical mollification. The proposed operator generates infinitely differentiable paths that strictly interpolate prescribed waypoints, converge to the original non-differentiable input with arbitrary precision, and satisfy explicit curvature bounds given by a closed-form expression, addressing the core requirements of path generation for controlled autonomous systems. We further establish a parametric family of path generation operators that contains both classical and directional mollification as special cases, providing a unifying theoretical framework for the systematic generation of smooth, feasible paths from non-differentiable planning outputs.

</details>


### [313] [Optimal Solutions for the Moving Target Vehicle Routing Problem with Obstacles via Lazy Branch and Price](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21880)
*Anoop Bhat,Geordan Gutow,Surya Singh,Zhongqiang Ren,Sivakumar Rathinam,Howie Choset*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Lazy BPRC方法，用于求解带障碍的移动目标车辆路径规划问题（MT-VRP-O），通过延迟计算轨迹真实成本并利用松弛连续性约束的下界加速优化过程，在保证最优解的同时显著提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分支定价法在求解MT-VRP-O时，每次求解受限主问题需精确计算每条路径的成本，而由于需进行避障和动态目标间的运动规划，成本计算开销大，导致整体效率低下。因此需要一种更高效的方法来降低计算负担。

Method: 提出Lazy Branch-and-Price with Relaxed Continuity（Lazy BPRC）方法，采用分支定价框架，通过引入松弛连续性约束的运动规划方法为路径成本提供下界估计，在受限主问题中使用这些下界以延迟真实成本的计算；仅在必要时才通过Graph of Convex Sets（GCS）上的最短路径搜索计算真实成本，并利用连续性松弛加速该搜索过程。

Result: 实验表明，Lazy BPRC相比两种消融版本，在求解MT-VRP-O时速度提升可达一个数量级，显著提高了求解效率，同时仍能保证获得最优解。

Conclusion: Lazy BPRC通过延迟精确成本计算和引入松弛连续性约束，有效降低了MT-VRP-O求解过程中的计算开销，是解决此类复杂动态路径规划问题的一种高效且最优的方法。

Abstract: The Moving Target Vehicle Routing Problem with Obstacles (MT-VRP-O) seeks trajectories for several agents that collectively intercept a set of moving targets. Each target has one or more time windows where it must be visited, and the agents must avoid static obstacles and satisfy speed and capacity constraints. We introduce Lazy Branch-and-Price with Relaxed Continuity (Lazy BPRC), which finds optimal solutions for the MT-VRP-O. Lazy BPRC applies the branch-and-price framework for VRPs, which alternates between a restricted master problem (RMP) and a pricing problem. The RMP aims to select a sequence of target-time window pairings (called a tour) for each agent to follow, from a limited subset of tours. The pricing problem adds tours to the limited subset. Conventionally, solving the RMP requires computing the cost for an agent to follow each tour in the limited subset. Computing these costs in the MT-VRP-O is computationally intensive, since it requires collision-free motion planning between moving targets. Lazy BPRC defers cost computations by solving the RMP using lower bounds on the costs of each tour, computed via motion planning with relaxed continuity constraints. We lazily evaluate the true costs of tours as-needed. We compute a tour's cost by searching for a shortest path on a Graph of Convex Sets (GCS), and we accelerate this search using our continuity relaxation method. We demonstrate that Lazy BPRC runs up to an order of magnitude faster than two ablations.

</details>


### [314] [Disengagement Analysis and Field Tests of a Prototypical Open-Source Level 4 Autonomous Driving System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21926)
*Marvin Seegert,Christian Oefinger,Korbinian Moller,Christoph Bank,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于Autoware的开源自动驾驶系统在236公里混合交通中的表现，通过新型五级关键性框架分析30次脱离，发现空间脱离率为0.127次/公里，主要原因为感知和规划模块的问题，揭示了标准指标难以发现的鲁棒性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源L4自动驾驶系统的实际能力在长距离真实交通中尚未充分探索，现有评估多依赖于厂商报告的脱离数据，缺乏对系统薄弱环节的深入分析。

Method: 使用搭载Autoware软件栈的研究车辆完成236公里混合交通测试，记录并基于提出的五级关键性框架对26次行程中的30次脱离事件进行分类分析。

Result: 空间脱离率为0.127 1/km，脱离多发生在低速、靠近静态物体和红绿灯处；40%源于感知失败（如目标跟踪丢失），26.7%源于规划失败（如因停放车辆导致死锁）；安全驾驶员存在频繁非必要干预，反映对系统信任不足。

Conclusion: 尽管开源软件支持大规模运行，但脱离分析对于发现标准性能指标无法捕捉的系统鲁棒性问题至关重要，未来需优化感知与规划模块并提升人机信任。

Abstract: Proprietary Autonomous Driving Systems are typically evaluated through disengagements, unplanned manual interventions to alter vehicle behavior, as annually reported by the California Department of Motor Vehicles. However, the real-world capabilities of prototypical open-source Level 4 vehicles over substantial distances remain largely unexplored. This study evaluates a research vehicle running an Autoware-based software stack across 236 km of mixed traffic. By classifying 30 disengagements across 26 rides with a novel five-level criticality framework, we observed a spatial disengagement rate of 0.127 1/km. Interventions predominantly occurred at lower speeds near static objects and traffic lights. Perception and Planning failures accounted for 40% and 26.7% of disengagements, respectively, largely due to object-tracking losses and operational deadlocks caused by parked vehicles. Frequent, unnecessary interventions highlighted a lack of trust on the part of the safety driver. These results show that while open-source software enables extensive operations, disengagement analysis is vital for uncovering robustness issues missed by standard metrics.

</details>


### [315] [VP-VLA: Visual Prompting as an Interface for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22003)
*Zixuan Wang,Yuxin Chen,Yuqi Liu,Jinhui Ye,Pengguang Chen,Changsheng Lu,Shu Liu,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VP-VLA提出了一种双系统框架，通过结构化视觉提示解耦高层规划与底层控制，提升了视觉-语言-动作模型在空间精度和分布外场景中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将视觉和语言直接映射为控制信号，导致空间精度差、鲁棒性弱，尤其在分布外场景中表现不佳。

Method: 引入System 2 Planner进行任务分解并生成目标对象与位置的视觉提示（如十字线、边界框），再由System 1 Controller结合视觉接地训练目标生成精确动作。

Result: 在Robocasa-GR1-Tabletop和SimplerEnv上分别提升5%和8.3%的成功率，优于QwenOFT和GR00T-N1.6等基线方法。

Conclusion: VP-VLA通过结构化视觉提示实现了更可靠的空间定位与动作执行，增强了模型的可解释性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models typically map visual observations and linguistic instructions directly to robotic control signals. This "black-box" mapping forces a single forward pass to simultaneously handle instruction interpretation, spatial grounding, and low-level control, often leading to poor spatial precision and limited robustness in out-of-distribution scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose VP-VLA, a dual-system framework that decouples high-level reasoning and low-level execution via a structured visual prompting interface. Specifically, a "System 2 Planner" decomposes complex instructions into sub-tasks and identifies relevant target objects and goal locations. These spatial anchors are then overlaid directly onto visual observations as structured visual prompts, such as crosshairs and bounding boxes. Guided by these prompts and enhanced by a novel auxiliary visual grounding objective during training, a "System 1 Controller" reliably generates precise low-level execution motions. Experiments on the Robocasa-GR1-Tabletop benchmark and SimplerEnv simulation demonstrate that VP-VLA improves success rates by 5% and 8.3%, surpassing competitive baselines including QwenOFT and GR00T-N1.6.

</details>


### [316] [MEVIUS2: Practical Open-Source Quadruped Robot with Sheet Metal Welding and Multimodal Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22031)
*Kento Kawaharazuka,Keita Yoneda,Shintaro Inoue,Temma Suzuki,Jun Oda,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一款名为MEVIUS2的开源四足机器人，其尺寸接近波士顿动力的Spot，结构部件可通过电商平台购买，采用金属加工工艺实现大尺寸和高耐久性，并集成多种传感器以增强环境感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开源四足机器人多依赖3D打印，结构脆弱且难以放大；少数金属结构的机器人仍偏小且缺乏多模态感知传感器，实用性不足。因此需要开发一种更大、更耐用且具备良好感知能力的开源四足机器人平台。

Method: 采用钣金焊接与金属机械加工技术制造结构件，减少零件数量并提升结构强度；集成LiDAR和高动态范围相机等传感器；所有硬件、软件及训练环境均开源。

Result: 成功构建了与Spot相当尺寸的MEVIUS2机器人，实验验证其可在多种复杂地形中行走，并具备精细的环境感知能力。

Conclusion: MEVIUS2是一款高度实用、可扩展性强的开源四足机器人平台，推动了开源机器人在真实环境中应用的发展。

Abstract: Various quadruped robots have been developed to date, and thanks to reinforcement learning, they are now capable of traversing diverse types of rough terrain. In parallel, there is a growing trend of releasing these robot designs as open-source, enabling researchers to freely build and modify robots themselves. However, most existing open-source quadruped robots have been designed with 3D printing in mind, resulting in structurally fragile systems that do not scale well in size, leading to the construction of relatively small robots. Although a few open-source quadruped robots constructed with metal components exist, they still tend to be small in size and lack multimodal sensors for perception, making them less practical. In this study, we developed MEVIUS2, an open-source quadruped robot with a size comparable to Boston Dynamics' Spot, whose structural components can all be ordered through e-commerce services. By leveraging sheet metal welding and metal machining, we achieved a large, highly durable body structure while reducing the number of individual parts. Furthermore, by integrating sensors such as LiDARs and a high dynamic range camera, the robot is capable of detailed perception of its surroundings, making it more practical than previous open-source quadruped robots. We experimentally validated that MEVIUS2 can traverse various types of rough terrain and demonstrated its environmental perception capabilities. All hardware, software, and training environments can be obtained from Supplementary Materials or https://github.com/haraduka/mevius2.

</details>


### [317] [RAFL: Generalizable Sim-to-Real of Soft Robots with Residual Acceleration Field Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22039)
*Dong Heon Cho,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种残差加速度场学习（RAFL）框架，通过在基础模拟器上添加可迁移的单元级修正动力学场来提升软体机器人在不同形态下的仿真精度，尤其在稀疏观测下实现跨形状的零样本泛化和持续优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于仿真与现实之间的差距以及材料参数辨识中几何依赖效应的干扰，传统基于微分模拟的软体机器人设计在面对形态变化时存在仿真失准问题，尤其是在几何本身作为设计变量时更为严重。

Method: 提出残差加速度场学习（RAFL）框架，在基础物理模拟器上引入一个基于局部共享特征的、单元级的可学习残差动力学场，该模型不依赖全局网格结构，通过端到端训练利用稀疏标记点数据进行学习，并可在不同形态间迁移。

Result: 在sim-to-sim和sim-to-real实验中，RAFL在未见过的形态上实现了零样本性能提升，优于系统辨识方法（后者常出现负迁移），并支持在形态优化过程中持续精炼仿真模型。

Conclusion: RAFL通过引入可迁移的局部残差动力学场，有效缓解了因几何变化导致的仿真失准问题，提升了不同形态下软体机器人仿真的准确性与泛化能力，具有良好的实用潜力。

Abstract: Differentiable simulators enable gradient-based optimization of soft robots over material parameters, control, and morphology, but accurately modeling real systems remains challenging due to the sim-to-real gap. This issue becomes more pronounced when geometry is itself a design variable. System identification reduces discrepancies by fitting global material parameters to data; however, when constitutive models are misspecified or observations are sparse, identified parameters often absorb geometry-dependent effects rather than reflect intrinsic material behavior. More expressive constitutive models can improve accuracy but substantially increase computational cost, limiting practicality.
  We propose a residual acceleration field learning (RAFL) framework that augments a base simulator with a transferable, element-level corrective dynamics field. Operating on shared local features, the model is agnostic to global mesh topology and discretization. Trained end-to-end through a differentiable simulator using sparse marker observations, the learned residual generalizes across shapes. In both sim-to-sim and sim-to-real experiments, our method achieves consistent zero-shot improvements on unseen morphologies, while system identification frequently exhibits negative transfer. The framework also supports continual refinement, enabling simulation accuracy to accumulate during morphology optimization.

</details>


### [318] [Programming Manufacturing Robots with Imperfect AI: LLMs as Tuning Experts for FDM Print Configuration Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22118)
*Ekta U. Samani,Christopher G. Atkeson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的闭环优化框架，用于3D打印参数调优，相较于单次AI推荐显著提升了成功率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D打印配置方法（如默认设置、试错或通用AI建议）难以可靠满足特定质量目标，缺乏基于证据的迭代优化能力。

Method: 将大语言模型作为调参专家嵌入贝叶斯优化循环中：每次打印后由评估器生成结构化诊断，LLM据此提出自然语言调整建议，并转化为可执行的机器指令进行下一轮优化。

Result: 在100个Thingi10k模型上测试，该方法在78%的对象上找到最优配置，失败率为0%；而单次AI推荐极少达到最优，且有15%的潜在失败率。

Conclusion: 大语言模型作为受限决策模块在数据驱动的优化循环中比作为端到端配置选择工具更有价值，该范式可推广至更广泛的机器人编程任务。

Abstract: We use fused deposition modeling (FDM) 3D printing as a case study of how manufacturing robots can use imperfect AI to acquire process expertise. In FDM, print configuration strongly affects output quality. Yet, novice users typically rely on default configurations, trial-and-error, or recommendations from generic AI models (e.g., ChatGPT). These strategies can produce complete prints, but they do not reliably meet specific objectives. Experts iteratively tune print configurations using evidence from prior prints. We present a modular closed-loop approach that treats an LLM as a source of tuning expertise. We embed this source of expertise within a Bayesian optimization loop. An approximate evaluator scores each print configuration and returns structured diagnostics, which the LLM uses to propose natural-language adjustments that are compiled into machine-actionable guidance for optimization. On 100 Thingi10k parts, our LLM-guided loop achieves the best configuration on 78% objects with 0% likely-to-fail cases, while single-shot AI model recommendations are rarely best and exhibit 15% likely-to-fail cases. These results suggest that LLMs provide more value as constrained decision modules in evidence-driven optimization loops than as end-to-end oracles for print configuration selection. We expect this result to extend to broader LLM-based robot programming.

</details>


### [319] [ROBOGATE: Adaptive Failure Discovery for Safe Robot Policy Deployment via Two-Stage Boundary-Focused Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22126)
*Byungjin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROBOGATE是一个结合物理仿真与两阶段自适应采样的机器人部署风险管控框架，能高效识别操作参数空间中的失效边界，支持工业级验证并揭示通用危险区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业环境中部署学习型机器人操作策略需要严格的预部署验证，但高维参数空间下的穷举测试不可行，因此需要一种高效的失效风险评估方法。

Method: 采用两阶段自适应采样策略：第一阶段使用拉丁超立方采样（LHS）在8维参数空间中进行2万次实验以建立粗略的失效分布；第二阶段在30%-70%成功率的过渡区集中进行1万次边界聚焦采样，结合物理仿真（NVIDIA Isaac Sim）和逻辑回归模型精确定位失效边界，并推广至VLA模型评估。

Result: 在Franka Panda和UR5e机器人上完成3万次实验，逻辑回归模型AUC达0.780（优于仅用第一阶段数据的0.754），成功推导出闭式失效边界方程并识别出影响两类机器人的四个通用危险区域；在VLA模型测试中，Octo-Small在68个对抗场景中成功率为0%，显著低于脚本基线的100%。

Conclusion: ROBOGATE能够高效、精确地识别机器人操作中的失效边界，为工业部署提供了可扩展的风险管理方案，且开源实现在单GPU工作站上即可运行。

Abstract: Deploying learned robot manipulation policies in industrial settings requires rigorous pre-deployment validation, yet exhaustive testing across high-dimensional parameter spaces is intractable. We present ROBOGATE, a deployment risk management framework that combines physics-based simulation with a two-stage adaptive sampling strategy to efficiently discover failure boundaries in the operational parameter space. Stage 1 employs Latin Hypercube Sampling (LHS) across an 8-dimensional parameter space to establish a coarse failure landscape from 20,000 uniformly distributed experiments. Stage 2 applies boundary-focused sampling that concentrates 10,000 additional experiments in the 30-70% success rate transition zone, enabling precise failure boundary mapping. Using NVIDIA Isaac Sim with Newton physics, we evaluate a scripted pick-and-place controller on two robot embodiments -- Franka Panda (7-DOF) and UR5e (6-DOF) -- across 30,000 total experiments. Our logistic regression risk model achieves an AUC of 0.780 on the combined dataset (vs. 0.754 for Stage 1 alone), identifies a closed-form failure boundary equation, and reveals four universal danger zones affecting both robot platforms. We further demonstrate the framework on VLA (Vision-Language-Action) model evaluation, where Octo-Small achieves 0.0% success rate on 68 adversarial scenarios versus 100% for the scripted baseline -- a 100-point gap that underscores the challenge of deploying foundation models in industrial settings. ROBOGATE is open-source and runs on a single GPU workstation.

</details>


### [320] [Closed-Loop Verbal Reinforcement Learning for Task-Level Robotic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22169)
*Dmitrii Plotnikov,Iaroslav Kolomiets,Dmitrii Maliukov,Dmitrij Kosenkov,Daniia Zinniatullina,Artem Trandofilov,Georgii Gazaryan,Kirill Bogatikov,Timofei Kozlov,Igor Duchinskii,Mikhail Konenkov,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的基于语言反馈的闭环强化学习框架VRL，用于在执行不确定条件下实现移动机器人系统的可解释任务级规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的强化学习方法难以提供透明和可解释的策略更新，特别是在复杂、动态的真实机器人系统中。需要一种能够在符号层面直接进行策略优化且支持人类理解与干预的方法。

Method: 提出Verbal Reinforcement Learning（VRL）框架，采用由Large Language Model（LLM）作为策略执行者、Vision-Language Model（VLM）作为评论者的闭环架构；通过自然语言反馈迭代优化Behavior Trees形式的可执行策略，无需梯度优化。

Result: 在真实移动机器人上验证了该框架的有效性，能够完成多阶段操作与导航任务；实验表明系统可实现可解释的策略改进、对执行失败的闭环适应，并稳定部署于物理平台。

Conclusion: VRL框架实现了无需梯度的、基于符号表达的任务规划与策略更新，支持透明推理与人类可读的反馈机制，为真实环境中可解释机器人决策提供了有效路径。

Abstract: We propose a new Verbal Reinforcement Learning (VRL) framework for interpretable task-level planning in mobile robotic systems operating under execution uncertainty. The framework follows a closed-loop architecture that enables iterative policy improvement through interaction with the physical environment. In our framework, executable Behavior Trees are repeatedly refined by a Large Language Model actor using structured natural-language feedback produced by a Vision-Language Model critic that observes the physical robot and execution traces. Unlike conventional reinforcement learning, policy updates in VRL occur directly at the symbolic planning level, without gradient-based optimization. This enables transparent reasoning, explicit causal feedback, and human-interpretable policy evolution. We validate the proposed framework on a real mobile robot performing a multi-stage manipulation and navigation task under execution uncertainty. Experimental results show that the framework supports explainable policy improvements, closed-loop adaptation to execution failures, and reliable deployment on physical robotic systems.

</details>


### [321] [Cross-Modal Reinforcement Learning for Navigation with Degraded Depth Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22182)
*Omkar Sawant,Luca Zanatta,Grzegorz Malczyk,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用深度和灰度图像互补信息的跨模态学习框架，通过跨模态Wasserstein自编码器学习共享潜在表示，并结合强化学习策略实现鲁棒导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不良光照或反射表面等恶劣条件下，深度传感器性能下降，需要一种能够利用其他模态（如灰度图像）补偿深度信息的方法以实现稳健导航。

Method: 提出了Cross-Modal Wasserstein Autoencoder来学习深度和灰度图像之间的共享潜在表示，强制执行跨模态一致性；将学习到的表示与基于强化学习的策略相结合，用于无碰撞导航。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，该方法在严重深度退化情况下仍保持良好性能，并能成功迁移到真实环境中。

Conclusion: 所提出的跨模态学习框架有效利用了多模态数据的互补性，在深度传感器退化时仍能实现鲁棒的自主导航。

Abstract: This paper presents a cross-modal learning framework that exploits complementary information from depth and grayscale images for robust navigation. We introduce a Cross-Modal Wasserstein Autoencoder that learns shared latent representations by enforcing cross-modal consistency, enabling the system to infer depth-relevant features from grayscale observations when depth measurements are corrupted. The learned representations are integrated with a Reinforcement Learning-based policy for collision-free navigation in unstructured environments when depth sensors experience degradation due to adverse conditions such as poor lighting or reflective surfaces. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that our approach maintains robust performance under significant depth degradation and successfully transfers to real environments.

</details>


### [322] [Make Tracking Easy: Neural Motion Retargeting for Humanoid Whole-body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22201)
*Qingrui Zhao,Kaiyue Yang,Xiyu Wang,Shiqi Zhao,Yi Lu,Xinfang Zhang,Wei Yin,Qiu Shen,Xiao-Xiao Long,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为NMR的神经运动重定向框架，通过学习数据分布而非优化来解决人形机器人运动重定向中的非凸问题，有效消除了关节跳跃和自碰撞问题，并提升了下游控制策略的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于传统基于优化的重定向方法存在非凸性，容易陷入局部最优，导致运动中出现关节跳跃和自穿透等物理伪影，难以将人类动作平滑迁移到人形机器人上。

Method: 提出NMR框架，采用基于VAE的动作聚类将异构运动分组为潜在模式，并设计分层数据流水线CEPR，结合强化学习专家修复人类动作为机器人可行运动流形，最后使用非自回归CNN-Transformer模型进行全局时序建模。

Result: 在Unitree G1机器人上的实验表明，NMR相比现有方法显著减少自碰撞并消除关节跳跃，在武术、舞蹈等动态任务中表现出色，且生成的参考动作能加速下游全身控制策略的收敛。

Conclusion: NMR通过从学习数据分布的角度重构运动重定向问题，为弥合人类与机器人在运动形态上的鸿沟提供了可扩展的有效路径。

Abstract: Humanoid robots require diverse motor skills to integrate into complex environments, but bridging the kinematic and dynamic embodiment gap from human data remains a major bottleneck. We demonstrate through Hessian analysis that traditional optimization-based retargeting is inherently non-convex and prone to local optima, leading to physical artifacts like joint jumps and self-penetration. To address this, we reformulate the targeting problem as learning data distribution rather than optimizing optimal solutions, where we propose NMR, a Neural Motion Retargeting framework that transforms static geometric mapping into a dynamics-aware learned process. We first propose Clustered-Expert Physics Refinement (CEPR), a hierarchical data pipeline that leverages VAE-based motion clustering to group heterogeneous movements into latent motifs. This strategy significantly reduces the computational overhead of massively parallel reinforcement learning experts, which project and repair noisy human demonstrations onto the robot's feasible motion manifold. The resulting high-fidelity data supervises a non-autoregressive CNN-Transformer architecture that reasons over global temporal context to suppress reconstruction noise and bypass geometric traps. Experiments on the Unitree G1 humanoid across diverse dynamic tasks (e.g., martial arts, dancing) show that NMR eliminates joint jumps and significantly reduces self-collisions compared to state-of-the-art baselines. Furthermore, NMR-generated references accelerate the convergence of downstream whole-body control policies, establishing a scalable path for bridging the human-robot embodiment gap.

</details>


### [323] [DexDrummer: In-Hand, Contact-Rich, and Long-Horizon Dexterous Robot Drumming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22263)
*Hung-Chieh Fang,Amber Xie,Jennifer Grannen,Kenneth Llontop,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了DexDrummer，一个基于仿真到现实迁移的分层双手机械人打鼓框架，用于测试灵巧操作在复杂任务中的综合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了验证机器人在复杂、接触丰富且长周期任务中手部灵巧性的极限，作者提出将打鼓作为灵巧操作的新测试平台。

Method: 采用分层策略，结合轨迹规划与残差强化学习修正，并设计了以物体为中心的双手机器人控制框架，在仿真中训练并通过sim-to-real迁移应用到真实世界。

Result: 在仿真中，该方法在多鼓双手机械演奏任务中显著优于固定抓握策略；在真实场景中成功实现了歌曲演奏，F1得分为1.0。

Conclusion: DexDrummer展示了在包含动态调整、频繁接触和长时间协调的复杂任务中实现高水平灵巧操作的可行性，为未来灵巧机器人研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Performing in-hand, contact-rich, and long-horizon dexterous manipulation remains an unsolved challenge in robotics. Prior hand dexterity works have considered each of these three challenges in isolation, yet do not combine these skills into a single, complex task. To further test the capabilities of dexterity, we propose drumming as a testbed for dexterous manipulation. Drumming naturally integrates all three challenges: it involves in-hand control for stabilizing and adjusting the drumstick with the fingers, contact-rich interaction through repeated striking of the drum surface, and long-horizon coordination when switching between drums and sustaining rhythmic play. We present DexDrummer, a hierarchical object-centric bimanual drumming policy trained in simulation with sim-to-real transfer. The framework reduces the exploration difficulty of pure reinforcement learning by combining trajectory planning with residual RL corrections for fast transitions between drums. A dexterous manipulation policy handles contact-rich dynamics, guided by rewards that explicitly model both finger-stick and stick-drum interactions. In simulation, we show our policy can play two styles of music: multi-drum, bimanual songs and challenging, technical exercises that require increased dexterity. Across simulated bimanual tasks, our dexterous, reactive policy outperforms a fixed grasp policy by 1.87x across easy songs and 1.22x across hard songs F1 scores. In real-world tasks, we show song performance across a multi-drum setup. DexDrummer is able to play our training song and its extended version with an F1 score of 1.0.

</details>


### [324] [UniDex: A Robot Foundation Suite for Universal Dexterous Hand Control from Egocentric Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22264)
*Gu Zhang,Qicheng Xu,Haozhe Zhang,Jianhan Ma,Long He,Yiming Bao,Zeyu Ping,Zhecheng Yuan,Chenhao Lu,Chengbo Yuan,Tianhai Liang,Xiaoyu Tian,Maanping Shao,Feihong Zhang,Mingyu Ding,Yang Gao,Hao Zhao,Hang Zhao,Huazhe Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniDex是一个面向通用灵巧手控制的机器人基础套件，包含大规模机器人中心数据集、统一的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略和实用的人类数据采集设置，在跨手泛化和工具使用任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作因真实机器人遥操作数据采集成本高、手部形态异构以及控制维度高等问题而具有挑战性，亟需一种通用且可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 构建了包含5万条轨迹的UniDex-Dataset，采用人机协同的指尖轨迹重定向方法，利用显式3D点云并掩码人手以缩小运动学与视觉差距；提出功能-执行器对齐空间（FAAS）作为统一动作空间，实现跨手迁移；训练基于3D点云的VLA策略，并开发便携式UniDex-Cap采集装置用于人类数据转机器人可执行轨迹。

Result: 在两种不同灵巧手上的复杂工具使用任务中，UniDex-VLA平均任务进度达81%，显著优于先前VLA基线，展现出强空间、物体及零样本跨手泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniDex-Dataset、UniDex-VLA与UniDex-Cap共同构成一个可扩展的基础套件，为通用灵巧操作提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation remains challenging due to the cost of collecting real-robot teleoperation data, the heterogeneity of hand embodiments, and the high dimensionality of control. We present UniDex, a robot foundation suite that couples a large-scale robot-centric dataset with a unified vision-language-action (VLA) policy and a practical human-data capture setup for universal dexterous hand control. First, we construct UniDex-Dataset, a robot-centric dataset over 50K trajectories across eight dexterous hands (6--24 DoFs), derived from egocentric human video datasets. To transform human data into robot-executable trajectories, we employ a human-in-the-loop retargeting procedure to align fingertip trajectories while preserving plausible hand-object contacts, and we operate on explicit 3D pointclouds with human hands masked to narrow kinematic and visual gaps. Second, we introduce the Function-Actuator-Aligned Space (FAAS), a unified action space that maps functionally similar actuators to shared coordinates, enabling cross-hand transfer. Leveraging FAAS as the action parameterization, we train UniDex-VLA, a 3D VLA policy pretrained on UniDex-Dataset and finetuned with task demonstrations. In addition, we build UniDex-Cap, a simple portable capture setup that records synchronized RGB-D streams and human hand poses and converts them into robot-executable trajectories to enable human-robot data co-training that reduces reliance on costly robot demonstrations. On challenging tool-use tasks across two different hands, UniDex-VLA achieves 81% average task progress and outperforms prior VLA baselines by a large margin, while exhibiting strong spatial, object, and zero-shot cross-hand generalization. Together, UniDex-Dataset, UniDex-VLA, and UniDex-Cap provide a scalable foundation suite for universal dexterous manipulation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [325] [ProMAS: Proactive Error Forecasting for Multi-Agent Systems Using Markov Transition Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20260)
*Xinkui Zhao,Sai Liu,Yifan Zhang,Qingyu Ma,Guanjie Cheng,Naibo Wang,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PROMAS是一种利用马尔可夫转移进行预测性错误分析的前瞻性框架，通过提取因果增量特征并建模为量化向量马尔可夫空间中的概率转移，实现对多智能体系统中推理错误的早期定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多依赖事后故障分析，难以实现实时干预；而多智能体系统中的逻辑错误容易快速传播，导致系统性失效，因此需要一种能够提前预测并定位错误的机制。

Method: 提出PROMAS框架，提取因果Delta特征以捕捉语义偏移，并将其映射到量化向量马尔可夫空间中建模推理过程为概率转移；结合前瞻性预测头与跳跃检测机制，通过风险加速而非静态阈值来定位错误。

Result: 在Who&When基准上，PROMAS仅处理27%的推理日志即达到22.97%的步级准确率，性能媲美MASC等反应式监控方法，数据开销降低73%，显著减少了干预延迟。

Conclusion: PROMAS在诊断精度与实时性之间取得平衡，虽存在一定准确率权衡，但显著提升了多智能体系统中自主推理的实时干预能力。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models into Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) has enabled the so-lution of complex, long-horizon tasks through collaborative reasoning. However, this collec-tive intelligence is inherently fragile, as a single logical fallacy can rapidly propagate and lead to system-wide failure. Most current research re-lies on post-hoc failure analysis, thereby hinder-ing real-time intervention. To address this, we propose PROMAS, a proactive framework utiliz-ing Markov transitions for predictive error anal-ysis. PROMAS extracts Causal Delta Features to capture semantic displacement, mapping them to a quantized Vector Markov Space to model reasoning as probabilistic transitions. By inte-grating a Proactive Prediction Head with Jump Detection, the method localizes errors via risk acceleration rather than static thresholds. On the Who&When benchmark, PROMAS achieves 22.97% step-level accuracy while processing only 27% of reasoning logs. This performance rivals reactive monitors like MASC while reducing data overhead by 73%. Although this strategy entails an accuracy trade-off compared to post-hoc meth-ods, it significantly improves intervention latency, balancing diagnostic precision with the real-time demands of autonomous reasoning.

</details>


### [326] [Domain-Specialized Tree of Thought through Plug-and-Play Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20267)
*Xuanqi Gao,Haoyu Wang,Jun Sun,Shiqing Ma,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级、可插拔的预测器DST，用于改进树状思维（ToT）框架中的搜索过程，在保持甚至提升推理准确率的同时显著降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ToT方法在探索深度与计算效率之间存在权衡，依赖重模型自评估或刚性启发式剪枝，导致计算成本高且灵活性差。

Method: 提出DST预测器，作为一种轻量级、有监督的启发式方法，实现动态、上下文感知的分支剪枝，简单步骤接近贪心搜索效率，复杂任务则自适应扩展搜索宽度。

Result: 在数学推理、通用推理和复杂逻辑推理等多个基准上验证，准确性与强基线相当或更优，计算开销减少26%-75%。

Conclusion: DST有效解决了基于树的推理中准确性和效率之间的权衡，使ToT从资源密集型技术转变为可扩展、实用的复杂问题求解范式。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced complex reasoning, prominent methods like the Tree of Thoughts (ToT) framework face a critical trade-off between exploration depth and computational efficiency. Existing ToT implementations often rely on heavyweight LLM-based self-evaluation or rigid heuristics for branch pruning, making them prohibitively expensive and inflexible for broad application. To address this, we introduce DST, an adaptable, plug-and-play predictor that serves as a lightweight, supervised heuristic to guide the ToT search process. Our predictor enables dynamic, context-aware pruning, allowing the search to proceed with near-greedy efficiency on simpler reasoning steps while adaptively expanding the search beam only when encountering uncertainty or task complexity. We evaluate our approach on a diverse suite of benchmarks spanning mathematical reasoning, general reasoning, and complex logical reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves accuracy competitive with or superior to strong baselines, including standard ToT, while reducing computational overhead by 26-75%. Our work effectively resolves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off in tree-based reasoning, transforming ToT from a resource-intensive technique into a scalable and practical paradigm for complex problem-solving in LLMs.

</details>


### [327] [Me, Myself, and $π$ : Evaluating and Explaining LLM Introspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20276)
*Atharv Naphade,Samarth Bhargav,Sean Lim,Mcnair Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种原则性分类法和名为Introspect-Bench的评估套件，用以严格测试大语言模型的内省能力，并发现前沿模型能通过注意力扩散机制实现对自身策略的特许访问与预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对大语言模型内省能力的评估难以区分真正的元认知与基于世界知识或文本模拟的行为，因此需要更严谨的框架来识别和衡量真正的内省能力。

Method: 提出了一个形式化的内省分类法，将其定义为模型策略和参数上的算子运算，并设计了多方面的评估套件Introspect-Bench；通过因果和机制分析研究内省能力的形成过程。

Result: 前沿大语言模型在预测自身行为方面优于同类模型，表现出对其自身策略的特权访问能力；研究发现了内省机制通过注意力扩散自然涌现的证据。

Conclusion: 大语言模型具备无需显式训练即可发展的内省能力，其机制可通过注意力扩散解释，表明真正的元认知在模型中可以自发产生。

Abstract: A hallmark of human intelligence is Introspection-the ability to assess and reason about one's own cognitive processes. Introspection has emerged as a promising but contested capability in large language models (LLMs). However, current evaluations often fail to distinguish genuine meta-cognition from the mere application of general world knowledge or text-based self-simulation. In this work, we propose a principled taxonomy that formalizes introspection as the latent computation of specific operators over a model's policy and parameters. To isolate the components of generalized introspection, we present Introspect-Bench, a multifaceted evaluation suite designed for rigorous capability testing. Our results show that frontier models exhibit privileged access to their own policies, outperforming peer models in predicting their own behavior. Furthermore, we provide causal, mechanistic evidence explaining both how LLMs learn to introspect without explicit training, and how the mechanism of introspection emerges via attention diffusion.

</details>


### [328] [AgentComm-Bench: Stress-Testing Cooperative Embodied AI Under Latency, Packet Loss, and Bandwidth Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20285)
*Aayam Bansal,Ishaan Gangwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentComm-Bench是一个用于评估具身AI在通信受限条件下协作性能的基准套件，涵盖六种通信损伤维度和三类任务，揭示了现有方法在真实通信环境下的脆弱性，并提出了一种基于冗余消息编码的轻量级缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的机器人、自动驾驶车辆和无人机群面临通信延迟、丢包和带宽限制等挑战，而现有协作多智能体方法大多在理想化通信假设下评估，缺乏对实际通信损伤的系统性测试。

Method: 设计并实现了AgentComm-Bench，包含六个通信损伤维度（延迟、丢包、带宽崩溃、异步更新、过时记忆、传感器证据冲突），应用于三类任务（协作感知、多智能体导航、协作区域搜索），评估五种通信策略，并提出一种结合冗余消息编码与过时信息感知融合的轻量级方法。

Result: 实验显示，在80%丢包情况下，冗余消息编码使导航性能提升一倍以上；过时记忆和带宽崩溃导致导航性能下降超过96%；内容污染使感知F1下降超85%；不同任务对不同类型损伤敏感度不同，例如感知融合对丢包鲁棒但会放大错误数据影响。

Conclusion: 协作具身AI必须在多种通信损伤条件下进行评估，AgentComm-Bench提供了一个实用的评估协议，建议未来研究应报告在多种非理想通信条件下的性能表现。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent methods for embodied AI are almost universally evaluated under idealized communication: zero latency, no packet loss, and unlimited bandwidth. Real-world deployment on robots with wireless links, autonomous vehicles on congested networks, or drone swarms in contested spectrum offers no such guarantees. We introduce AgentComm-Bench, a benchmark suite and evaluation protocol that systematically stress-tests cooperative embodied AI under six communication impairment dimensions: latency, packet loss, bandwidth collapse, asynchronous updates, stale memory, and conflicting sensor evidence. AgentComm-Bench spans three task families: cooperative perception, multi-agent waypoint navigation, and cooperative zone search, and evaluates five communication strategies, including a lightweight method we propose based on redundant message coding with staleness-aware fusion. Our experiments reveal that communication-dependent tasks degrade catastrophically: stale memory and bandwidth collapse cause over 96% performance drops in navigation, while content corruption (stale or conflicting data) reduces perception F1 by over 85%. Vulnerability depends on the interaction between impairment type and task design; perception fusion is robust to packet loss but amplifies corrupted data. Redundant message coding more than doubles navigation performance under 80% packet loss. We release AgentComm-Bench as a practical evaluation protocol and recommend that cooperative embodied AI work report performance under multiple impairment conditions.

</details>


### [329] [LLM-Enhanced Energy Contrastive Learning for Out-of-Distribution Detection in Text-Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20293)
*Xiaoxu Ma,Dong Li,Minglai Shao,Xintao Wu,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于文本属性图中节点级分布外（OOD）检测的新方法LECT，结合大语言模型（LLM）和基于能量的对比学习，生成高质量伪OOD样本并有效区分分布内与分布外节点，在六个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理文本属性图时假设训练和测试数据分布一致，难以应对实际场景中的分布外数据，导致性能下降，因此需要更鲁棒的OOD检测机制。

Method: 提出LECT方法，利用大语言模型的语义理解能力生成依赖关系感知的伪OOD节点，并结合能量函数进行对比学习，以增强对OOD节点的识别能力。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的实验表明，LECT在节点分类准确率和OOD检测性能上均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: LECT通过融合LLM与能量对比学习，有效提升了文本属性图中OOD检测的鲁棒性和准确性，具有良好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Text-attributed graphs, where nodes are enriched with textual attributes, have become a powerful tool for modeling real-world networks such as citation, social, and transaction networks. However, existing methods for learning from these graphs often assume that the distributions of training and testing data are consistent. This assumption leads to significant performance degradation when faced with out-of-distribution (OOD) data. In this paper, we address the challenge of node-level OOD detection in text-attributed graphs, with the goal of maintaining accurate node classification while simultaneously identifying OOD nodes. We propose a novel approach, LLM-Enhanced Energy Contrastive Learning for Out-of-Distribution Detection in Text-Attributed Graphs (LECT), which integrates large language models (LLMs) and energy-based contrastive learning. The proposed method involves generating high-quality OOD samples by leveraging the semantic understanding and contextual knowledge of LLMs to create dependency-aware pseudo-OOD nodes, and applying contrastive learning based on energy functions to distinguish between in-distribution (IND) and OOD nodes. The effectiveness of our method is demonstrated through extensive experiments on six benchmark datasets, where our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving both high classification accuracy and robust OOD detection capabilities.

</details>


### [330] [Compression is all you need: Modeling Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20396)
*Vitaly Aksenov,Eve Bodnia,Michael H. Freedman,Michael Mulligan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 人类数学（HM）是形式数学（FM）中极小但具有高度可压缩性的子集，通过层级化定义和定理实现表达力的指数扩展；基于MathLib数据分析表明其符合交换幺半群模型（A_n），支持HM位于FM中多项式增长的可压缩区域的论点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨人类数学为何仅覆盖形式数学极小部分，并揭示其区别于全量形式系统的本质特征——可压缩性与层级结构。

Method: 使用交换与非交换自由幺半群（A_n 和 F_n）建模数学推导中的宏压缩机制，并分析MathLib库中定义深度、包装长度与展开长度之间的关系以验证模型。

Result: 发现展开长度随深度和包装长度呈指数增长，而包装长度在各深度近似恒定，结果符合A_n模型，不符F_n模型，表明人类数学具有高度可压缩性。

Conclusion: 人类数学之所以特殊，在于其通过层级化结构实现高效压缩，处于形式数学中可压缩且兴趣密集的区域，该特性可用于指导自动化推理系统聚焦有价值的内容。

Abstract: Human mathematics (HM), the mathematics humans discover and value, is a vanishingly small subset of formal mathematics (FM), the totality of all valid deductions. We argue that HM is distinguished by its compressibility through hierarchically nested definitions, lemmas, and theorems. We model this with monoids. A mathematical deduction is a string of primitive symbols; a definition or theorem is a named substring or macro whose use compresses the string. In the free abelian monoid $A_n$, a logarithmically sparse macro set achieves exponential expansion of expressivity. In the free non-abelian monoid $F_n$, even a polynomially-dense macro set only yields linear expansion; superlinear expansion requires near-maximal density. We test these models against MathLib, a large Lean~4 library of mathematics that we take as a proxy for HM. Each element has a depth (layers of definitional nesting), a wrapped length (tokens in its definition), and an unwrapped length (primitive symbols after fully expanding all references). We find unwrapped length grows exponentially with both depth and wrapped length; wrapped length is approximately constant across all depths. These results are consistent with $A_n$ and inconsistent with $F_n$, supporting the thesis that HM occupies a polynomially-growing subset of the exponentially growing space FM. We discuss how compression, measured on the MathLib dependency graph, and a PageRank-style analysis of that graph can quantify mathematical interest and help direct automated reasoning toward the compressible regions where human mathematics lives.

</details>


### [331] [Leveraging Natural Language Processing and Machine Learning for Evidence-Based Food Security Policy Decision-Making in Data-Scarce Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20425)
*Karan Kumar Singh,Nikita Gajbhiye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为ZeroHungerAI的NLP与ML集成框架，用于在极端数据稀缺条件下进行基于证据的粮食安全政策建模，结合社会经济指标与文本嵌入，在小样本不平衡数据上实现了高精度预测，并提升了模型公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于结构化数据缺乏、文本报告碎片化以及决策系统中的群体偏见，数据匮乏地区的粮食安全政策制定面临重大挑战，亟需一种能在低资源环境下实现精准且公平预测的方法。

Method: 采用基于迁移学习的DistilBERT架构，融合结构化社会经济指标与政策文本的上下文嵌入，构建ZeroHungerAI框架，并引入公平性优化策略以减少城乡等群体间的预测偏差。

Result: 在1200个样本、25个地区的混合数据集上，模型达到91%准确率、0.89精确率、0.85召回率和0.86 F1分数，较SVM和逻辑回归分别提升13%和17%，并对少数类具有良好的识别能力（平均精确率约0.88），公平性优化使人口统计差异降至3%。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的上下文学习能显著增强低资源治理环境下的政策智能，ZeroHungerAI为实现可扩展、无偏见的饥饿预测与粮食安全政策制定提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Food security policy formulation in data-scarce regions remains a critical challenge due to limited structured datasets, fragmented textual reports, and demographic bias in decision-making systems. This study proposes ZeroHungerAI, an integrated Natural Language Processing (NLP) and Machine Learning (ML) framework designed for evidence-based food security policy modeling under extreme data scarcity. The system combines structured socio-economic indicators with contextual policy text embeddings using a transfer learning based DistilBERT architecture. Experimental evaluation on a 1200-sample hybrid dataset across 25 districts demonstrates superior predictive performance, achieving 91 percent classification accuracy, 0.89 precision, 0.85 recall, and an F1 score of 0.86 under imbalanced conditions. Comparative analysis shows a 13 percent performance improvement over classical SVM and 17 percent over Logistic Regression models. Precision Recall evaluation confirms robust minority class detection (average precision around 0.88). Fairness aware optimization reduces demographic parity difference to 3 percent, ensuring equitable rural urban policy inference. The results validate that transformer based contextual learning significantly enhances policy intelligence in low resource governance environments, enabling scalable and bias aware hunger prediction systems.

</details>


### [332] [Efficient Counterfactual Reasoning in ProbLog via Single World Intervention Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20505)
*Saimun Habib,Vaishak Belle,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在ProbLog中实现反事实推理的高效程序转换方法SWIP，通过将程序分解为观测和固定部分，降低了计算复杂度，并在保持正确性的同时显著提升了推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实推理对于构建可靠、可信的AI系统至关重要，但在概率逻辑编程（PLP）中实现该功能常面临计算成本高和准确性不稳定的问题。本文旨在解决这一挑战。

Method: 提出Single World Intervention Programs (SWIPs) 方法，通过对ProbLog子句进行系统性拆分，分离出与反事实相关的观测和固定成分，将反事实推理简化为简化程序上的边际推断，并在较弱的独立性假设下证明其正确性。

Result: 该方法在不增加渐近复杂度的前提下，在常见情况下生成更小的程序，实验显示推理时间比现有方法减少35%，且结果与潜在因果模型的反事实分布一致。

Conclusion: SWIP使复杂的反事实推理在ProbLog中更高效、稳定，推动了可解释和鲁棒AI系统的发展。

Abstract: Probabilistic Logic Programming (PLP) languages, like ProbLog, naturally support reasoning under uncertainty, while maintaining a declarative and interpretable framework. Meanwhile, counterfactual reasoning (i.e., answering ``what if'' questions) is critical for ensuring AI systems are robust and trustworthy; however, integrating this capability into PLP can be computationally prohibitive and unstable in accuracy. This paper addresses this challenge, by proposing an efficient program transformation for counterfactuals as Single World Intervention Programs (SWIPs) in ProbLog. By systematically splitting ProbLog clauses to observed and fixed components relevant to a counterfactual, we create a transformed program that (1) does not asymptotically exceed the computational complexity of existing methods, and is strictly smaller in common cases, and (2) reduces counterfactual reasoning to marginal inference over a simpler program. We formally prove the correctness of our approach, which relies on a weaker set independence assumptions and is consistent with conditional independencies, showing the resulting marginal probabilities match the counterfactual distributions of the underlying Structural Causal Model in wide domains. Our method achieves a 35\% reduction in inference time versus existing methods in extensive experiments. This work makes complex counterfactual reasoning more computationally tractable and reliable, providing a crucial step towards developing more robust and explainable AI systems. The code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/swip.

</details>


### [333] [Grounded Chess Reasoning in Language Models via Master Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20510)
*Zhenwei Tang,Qianfeng Wen,Seth Grief-Albert,Yahya Elgabra,Blair Yang,Honghua Dong,Ashton Anderson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将专家系统推理过程蒸馏为自然语言思维链的方法，使小型模型在数据稀缺的专业领域中获得可解释的推理能力，在国际象棋任务中显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
